### [164] [MMHBench: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark for Mental Health Understanding in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27895)
*Jinpeng Hu,Erqiang Wang,Shan Wang,Zhuo Li,Peipei Song,Xun Yang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMHBench，一个用于长视频心理健康理解的多视角多模态基准测试，包含268个长视频和2184个精心设计的问题，涵盖第三人称评估和第一人称视角推理两种设置。通过多智能体问题生成框架合成多样化视角的问题，并评估22个多模态大语言模型，结果表明长视频心理健康理解仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康理解基准多将其简化为粗粒度分类任务，无法判断模型是真正理解心理现象还是依赖表面相关性。需要更全面、细粒度的基准来评估模型对长视频中心理健康的多视角理解能力。

Method: 1）构建MMHBench基准，包含268个长视频和2184个问题，分为第三人称评估（605题，关注可观察行为和多模态证据解读）与第一人称视角推理（1579题，需基于视角条件推理）；2）提出多智能体问题生成框架（MAQG），模拟多种社会角色合成多视角问题，通过多角色反馈和迭代优化精炼问题，并经专家引导验证确保质量。

Result: 对22个代表性多模态大语言模型（涵盖开源和领先闭源模型）的广泛评估表明，长视频心理健康理解仍然高度具有挑战性。

Conclusion: MMHBench填补了心理健康理解基准的空白，从第三人称和第一人称双视角系统评估多模态模型对长视频心理健康的理解能力，揭示了现有MLLMs在该任务上的显著局限，为后续研究提供了有价值的评估框架和基准。

Abstract: Mental health understanding in long-form videos requires nuanced reasoning over observable behavior, interpersonal context, and latent psychological states. Existing benchmarks largely reduce this task to coarse-grained classification, providing limited insight into whether models truly understand psychological phenomena or rely on superficial correlations. To address this limitation, we introduce MMHBench, a comprehensive multimodal benchmark for multi-perspective mental health understanding, comprising 268 long-form videos and 2,184 carefully curated questions. MMHBench organizes the evaluation into two complementary settings: (1) third-person assessment, consisting of 605 questions that focus on the interpretation of observable behaviors and multimodal evidence, and (2) first-person perspective-taking, comprising 1,579 questions that require perspective-conditioned reasoning to identify the interpretation of the mental state supported by the available multimodal evidence. We propose a Multi-Agent Question Generation (MAQG) framework that simulates diverse social roles to synthesize questions from multiple perspectives. The generated questions are refined through multi-role feedback and iterative optimization, followed by expert-guided verification to ensure quality and validity. Extensive evaluation of 22 representative multimodal large language models (MLLMs), spanning both open-source and leading closed-source models, demonstrates that long-form video mental health understanding remains highly challenging.

</details>


### [165] [A Cross-Architecture Audit of Direction-Based Inference-Time Defences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27910)
*Xiangyu Yin,Tora Bodin,Rohan Menon,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在统一量级控制协议下，对视觉语言模型（VLM）越狱防御的五种推理时残差流方向干预候选进行了系统性比较，覆盖四个架构家族、15个模型-层单元格。结果显示没有单一候选在拒绝恢复和实用性保持上全面占优：图像条件偏移在LLaVA 1.5和Pixtral 12B上领先且是唯一在各类家族中效用损失保持噪声本底的候选；提示指令在Qwen2.5 VL上领先；攻击特异性残差在Qwen2 VL 2B上领先。图像条件方向在13/15个单元格中方向特异，但架构特异且不可跨架构迁移。CMRM方向与图像条件偏移在所有单元格中均呈正余弦对齐（均值0.35，为随机向量零假设的15-25倍，符号检验p≈3e-5），表明两种防御配方恢复部分重叠的几何结构，且基于方向的防御需针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型面临越狱攻击威胁，推理时防御通常从选定解码器层的残差流中减去一个校准方向。然而，现有各类防御候选（如CMRM风格的拒绝方向、ShiftDC风格的攻击特定残差、图像条件偏移等）缺乏在统一协议下的公平比较，且其几何关系与跨架构可迁移性尚未被系统性研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为VLM推理时防御的方向选择提供实证指导。

Method: 采用量级控制协议（magnitude controlled protocol），为每个提示匹配干预大小，并将每个候选方向与同范数的随机对照配对比较。对五个防御候选（均值图像条件偏移、CMRM风格拒绝方向、ShiftDC风格攻击特异性残差、提示忽略图像的指令、随机对照）在四个架构家族、15个模型-层单元格中进行评估。同时通过余弦对齐分析和符号检验，考察文本仅模态与多模态拒绝几何之间的关联，以及不同防御方向之间的几何重叠。

Result: (1) 无单一候选在拒绝恢复与效用保持上全面占优；(2) 图像条件偏移在LLaVA 1.5和Pixtral 12B上领先，且是唯一在所有家族中效用损失处于测量噪声本底的候选；(3) 提示指令在Qwen2.5 VL上领先，攻击特异性残差在Qwen2 VL 2B上领先；(4) 图像条件方向在13/15个单元格中表现出方向特异性，但在唯一架构兼容对（LLaVA 1.5 13B与Pixtral 12B）之间不可迁移、强架构特异；(5) CMRM方向与图像条件偏移在所有15个单元格中均为正余弦对齐，均值0.35，范围0.17-0.65，为随机零假设的15-25倍，符号检验p值约3e-5。

Conclusion: 文本仅模态与多模态拒绝几何之间存在部分重叠的一致性关联；不同防御配方（CMRM与图像条件偏移）恢复了部分重叠的几何结构。基于方向的VLM防御不应跨架构直接迁移，需针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准；攻击特定残差、图像条件偏移与提示级防御的效能差异提示应结合具体模型架构选择防御策略。

Abstract: Inference time defences against vision language model jailbreaks often subtract a calibrated direction from the residual stream at a chosen decoder layer. We compare five defence candidates across 15 model and layer cells from four architectural families under a magnitude controlled protocol that matches the intervention size for each prompt and pairs every direction with a random control of the same norm. The candidates are the mean image conditioning shift, a CMRM style refusal direction, a ShiftDC style attack specific residual, a prompt instruction to ignore the image, and a random control. No single candidate dominates on both refusal recovery and utility preservation. The image conditioning shift leads on LLaVA 1.5 and Pixtral 12B and is the only candidate whose utility loss remains at the measurement noise floor in every family. The prompt instruction leads on Qwen2.5 VL, while the attack specific residual leads on Qwen2 VL 2B. The image conditioning direction is direction specific in 13 of 15 cells, but strongly architecture specific and nontransferable across the only dimension compatible pair, LLaVA 1.5 13B and Pixtral 12B. We also connect text only and multimodal refusal geometry. The CMRM direction has positive cosine alignment with the image conditioning shift in all 15 cells, with mean 0.35, range 0.17 to 0.65, 15 to 25 times the random vector null, and a sign test p value of about 3e-5. These results show that the two recipes recover partially overlapping geometry and that direction based defences should be calibrated separately for each language decoder family.

</details>


### [166] [IFHierBench: Hierarchical Instruction Following for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27912)
*Yuetian Mao,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IFHierBench，一个层次化指令遵循基准，包含600个提示，涵盖四种约束树深度和35种不同约束，每个提示配备确定性检查器。评估七个领先模型后发现，即使最强模型提示级准确率仅略超50%，且准确率随约束深度增加急剧下降，表明当前LLM在遵循嵌套约束方面仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循基准将约束集视为统一应用于整个响应的扁平列表，无法将检查范围限定到输出的特定部分。然而现代部署中，单个LLM调用需要处理具有层次结构的输出（整体工件、结构章节、嵌套字段），每层都有各自的约束需要满足，因此需要能够按作用域验证约束的基准。

Method: 构建IFHierBench基准，包含600个提示，跨越四种约束树深度和35种不同约束，每个提示配备确定性检查器，能够验证每个层级作用域上的约束满足情况。评估了七个领先的专有和开源权重模型。

Result: 最强模型在提示级准确率上仅略超50%，准确率随约束深度增长而急剧下降。当前LLM在可靠遵循嵌套约束方面仍存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 层次化约束遵循是当前LLM的主要薄弱点，需要未来训练方法在更细粒度上考虑约束遵循，以实现更好的指令遵循能力。

Abstract: Instruction-following ability is critical for deploying large language models in real-world applications, where downstream components depend on the output satisfying specific constraints. Modern deployments increasingly handle the full task in a single LLM call, with one prompt specifying a layered output whose overall artifact, structural sections, and nested fields must each satisfy concrete constraints. Existing instruction-following benchmarks treat the constraint set as a flat list applied uniformly to the response, so they cannot scope a check to a particular section of the output. We introduce IFHierBench, a hierarchical instruction-following benchmark of 600 prompts stratified across four constraint-tree depths and 35 distinct constraints, each prompt paired with a deterministic checker that verifies satisfaction at every scope. Evaluating seven leading proprietary and open-weight models, we find that even the strongest model only marginally exceeds 50% prompt-level accuracy and that accuracy degrades sharply as constraint depth grows. Reliably following nested constraints remains a substantial gap for current LLMs, motivating future training methods that consider constraint adherence at finer granularity to achieve better instruction-following ability.

</details>


### [167] [One Anchor for All: Unified Multilingual and Multimodal Safety Alignment for LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27917)
*Enyi Shi,Fei Shen,Chuancheng Shi,Linxia Zhu,Shuyi Miao,Jinhui Tang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模态与语言共享安全神经元（MLS-Neurons）的跨维度安全对齐框架，通过识别和微调极少量共享神经元（约0.03%参数），将仅英语的安全监督迁移到多语言和多模态场景，以防御组合攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在全球部署中面临多语言指令与视觉信息结合的组合攻击威胁，但现有防御方法将语言和模态防御割裂，加上安全数据稀缺和高微调成本，难以有效应对。

Method: 首先通过对比有害与良性样本的响应，基于激活强度和下游影响识别单语单模态安全神经元；然后通过单模态神经元的交集提取模态共享安全神经元（MS-Neurons）；以英语为语义锚点，跨语言取交集得到MLS-Neurons；最后仅更新这一最小共享子集（约0.03%参数）完成安全对齐。

Result: 在多个多语言和多模态安全基准上显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: 通过定位并微调极小规模共享安全神经元，可以有效实现从英语单模态安全监督到多语言多模态场景的安全迁移，为LVLMs的组合攻击防御提供了一条高效低成本的路径。

Abstract: As large vision-language models (LVLMs) are deployed globally, the combination of multilingual instructions and visual information makes malicious attacks more covert and sophisticated than ever before. However, existing methods isolate language and modality defenses, which, coupled with the scarcity of safety data and high fine-tuning costs, makes it difficult for models to defend against compound attacks. To address this severe challenge, we propose a neuron-level cross-dimensional safety alignment framework driven by modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons). First, we identify monolingual and unimodal safety neurons by comparing responses to harmful and benign samples, quantifying functional saliency through activation strength and downstream impact. Then, by intersecting these unimodal neurons within each language, we extract modality-shared safety neurons (MS-Neurons) responsive to both visual and textual risks, bridging the safety representation gap between modalities. Furthermore, using English as a semantic anchor, we intersect MS-Neurons across languages to identify modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons), serving as key defenses against compound attacks. Finally, we update only this minimal subset of shared neurons (~0.03% of parameters), transferring English-only safety supervision to multilingual and multimodal scenarios. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse multilingual and multimodal safety benchmarks while preserving general utility.

</details>


### [168] [Meta-Task: Turning Terminal Task Synthesis into a Terminal Task for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27929)
*Zhihong Pan,Jiyuan He,Kai Zhang,Yupeng Han,Ze Liu,Yuze Zhao,Yongcong Ye,Zhaohua Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta-Task框架，将终端任务合成重新定义为Terminal-Bench格式的任务本身：代理在真实容器环境中迭代生成、执行和验证任务，从而在生成循环内部检查合成组件的内部一致性和可执行性。仅在3,221条Meta-Task合成轨迹上微调，Qwen3-14B和Qwen3-32B分别达到22.5%和31.8%的Avg Pass@1，超越同类方法且数据量显著更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模训练终端代理需要多样化、可验证的终端任务和高品质交互轨迹，但现有数据合成方法存在两大局限：(1)任务生成与实际执行脱节导致可靠性不足；(2)依赖现有仓库导致多样性有限、难以扩展。

Method: Meta-Task将任务合成本身定义为Terminal-Bench格式的任务，由代理在真实容器环境中迭代生成、执行和验证任务，确保生成组件在循环内被检查一致性和可执行性。具体包括：沿多个维度解耦目标任务需求、引入多阶段机制动态设计新任务规格后再生成实际任务、融入可选外部材料支持以增强多样性和真实性，并应用LLM-as-Judge过滤确保最终训练数据质量。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，仅用3,221条Meta-Task合成轨迹微调，Qwen3-14B和Qwen3-32B分别达到22.5%和31.8%的Avg Pass@1，以显著更少的训练数据超越同期方法。

Conclusion: Meta-Task通过将任务合成过程本身转化为可执行、可验证的代理任务，解决了终端任务数据合成的可靠性和多样性难题，验证了该方法在数据效率上的显著优势。

Abstract: Training terminal agents at scale requires diverse, verifiable terminal tasks and high-quality interaction trajectories, yet acquiring such data remains a significant challenge. Existing synthesis methods face two key limitations: (1) weak reliability caused by the disconnect between task generation and real execution, and (2) limited diversity and scalability due to dependence on existing repositories. We propose Meta-Task, a framework that redefines terminal task synthesis as a Terminal-Bench-format task itself: an agent operates within a real container environment to iteratively generate, execute, and verify tasks, so that synthesized components are checked for internal consistency and executability within the generation loop itself. Building upon this, we decouple the target task requirements along multiple dimensions, introduce a multi-phase mechanism that dynamically designs novel task specifications before producing the actual tasks, and incorporate optional external material support to enhance diversity and realism. We additionally apply LLM-as-Judge filtering to ensure the quality of the final training data. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.0 show that fine-tuning on only 3,221 Meta-Task synthesized trajectories achieves 22.5% and 31.8% Avg Pass@1 for Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B respectively, outperforming concurrent approaches with significantly less training data.

</details>


### [169] [The Geometric Nature and a Free Proxy for Flow-Matching Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27933)
*Ziyang Rao,Yiren Zhao,Weiyu Guo,Ben Fei,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何解释的流匹配（FM）不确定性估计方法——denoising acceleration（accel），它从单次前向传播中去噪轨迹的弯曲程度来衡量模型不确定性，无需额外训练、重采样或计算开销，能够有效用于在线故障检测和OOD输入识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配已成为现代具身模型流行的动作头范式，但作为条件生成模型，它不显式暴露固有不确定性，即使在误解场景或遇到分布外输入时也会产生错误动作块。安全部署需要判断FM生成动作何时可信任，但现有不确定性估计方法存在额外训练预算、高计算开销和低泛化能力等问题。

Method: 作者在速度场中给出了FM不确定性的几何解释，将不确定性表征为对理想仿射-各向同性收缩场的偏离。基于此观察，引入denoising acceleration (accel)作为不确定性代理指标，通过测量单次前向传播中去噪轨迹的弯曲程度来估计不确定性，无需额外模型评估、训练或重采样。

Result: 理论和经验上均证明accel是FM不确定性的忠实代理，并在在线故障检测中验证了其实用性。结果表明，在现实部署预算下，accel能够在终止前很早地识别失败轨迹，匹配甚至超越昂贵的基于重采样和训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为流匹配不确定性估计提供了一种零成本、高泛化能力的解决方案，通过几何视角揭示了不确定性在去噪轨迹中的可观测信号，为具身模型的安全部署提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) has become a popular action head paradigm for modern embodied models. However, as a conditional generative model, it does not explicitly expose its inherent uncertainty, producing faulty action chunks even when it misinterprets the scene or encounters out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. Therefore, determining when an FM-generated action can be trusted is essential for safe deployment, yet existing uncertainty estimation methods on real-time control suffer from several issues: extra training budget, high computational overhead, and low generalization ability. In this work, we provide a geometric interpretation of FM uncertainty in the velocity field, showing that uncertainty manifests as deviation from an ideal affine-isotropic contraction field. Building on this observation, we introduce denoising acceleration ($\mathrm{accel}$), a highly-generalizable and cost-free uncertainty proxy that measures the bending of the denoising trajectory from a single forward pass, without additional model evaluations, training, or resampling. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that $\mathrm{accel}$ is a faithful proxy for FM uncertainty and further test its utility in online failure detection. Results show that $\mathrm{accel}$ identifies failing rollouts well before termination, matching or even outperforming costly resampling- and training-based baselines across settings under realistic deployment budget. Code and demos available at: https://github.com/rrrrrrzy/fm-geometry.

</details>


### [170] [Shapes from Examples: Foundations of Shape Learning in Recursive SHACL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27934)
*Bente Gortworst,Cem Okulmus,Magdalena Ortiz,Anni-Yasmin Turhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了SHACL形状学习中基于拟合的方法：给定正负示例节点集合，计算一个用描述逻辑ELI核心片段表述的形状表达式（可包含递归形状目录），使其验证所有正示例且排除所有负示例。作者给出了拟合存在性和最具体拟合计算问题的时间复杂度上界，并分析了相关特例的多项式复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHACL形状用于数据图验证，自动学习形状对知识图谱应用至关重要。本文旨在解决形状学习中的拟合问题，即给定正负示例节点，自动推导出能够正确分类这些节点的形状表达式。

Method: 将SHACL形状表达式限定在对应描述逻辑ELI的核心片段中，并考虑递归形状目录的三种语义（良基、稳定和支持语义）。针对拟合存在性和最具体拟合计算两个问题，建立了紧的指数时间上界，并分析了可多项式求解的特例。

Result: 建立了拟合存在性和最具体拟合计算两个问题的紧指数时间上界，同时对相关特例得到了多项式时间复杂度的结果。

Conclusion: 在ELI对应的SHACL核心片段中，形状拟合问题在最一般情形下具有指数时间复杂度，但在某些特例下可以实现多项式复杂度，为实际知识图谱应用中的自动形状学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: SHACL shapes enable data graph validation, making automatic shape learning essential for knowledge graph applications. We investigate the well-known fitting approach to this task: given sets P and N of positive and negative example nodes from an input graph, compute a shape expression C, possibly using shape names defined in a recursive shape catalogue, that validates at every node in P and none in N. We focus on the case where C is written in a core fragment of SHACL corresponding to the Description Logic ELI. For the catalogue, we consider the well-founded, stable, and supported semantics. We address fitting existence and most specific fitting computation, establish tight exponential-time upper bounds for both problems, and obtain polynomial bounds for relevant special cases.

</details>


### [171] [From Scoring to Acting: Outcome-Verified Comparative Self-Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27937)
*Xu Xia,Jinghua Piao,Min Yang,Xiaochong Lan,Jiaju Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种"结果验证的比较式自蒸馏"（OVCSD）方法，通过对学生失败轨迹构建前缀树、从学生已达状态调用技能条件教师模型、仅保留经过环境结果验证的成功延续，并在首个状态对齐分歧处进行局部比较学习来蒸馏教师后缀，从而将技能内化到LLM智能体中，在ALFWorld和WebShop上相较最强基线分别提升29.7和5.4个绝对成功率点，且训练期间特权交互开销不足3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的自蒸馏方法存在两大局限：一是教师偏好未经过环境结果的验证，导致监督信号不可靠；二是仅基于学生轨迹上的行为级分数进行监督，未能充分利用学生轨迹、教师轨迹及其行为关系中的丰富信息。

Method: OVCSD包括三个关键步骤：(1) 将失败的学生轨迹组织成前缀树，识别出学生遇到困难的共同状态；(2) 从这些学生已达状态自适应地调用技能条件教师模型，生成延续行为；(3) 仅保留经过环境结果验证的成功延续，并在学生-教师轨迹的首个状态对齐分歧处进行局部比较学习（对比正负样本），再蒸馏分歧后的教师后缀以完整转移完成行为。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个基准上、三种模型规模下进行实验，OVCSD一致地优于无技能强化学习和现有自蒸馏基线，在ALFWorld上比最强基线高29.7个绝对成功率点，在WebShop上高5.4个绝对成功率点，且训练期间增加的特权交互不到3%。

Conclusion: 结果验证的教师监督与基于轨迹的对比学习是有效且高效的自蒸馏范式，能够在训练时以极小的特权交互代价显著提升LLM智能体的技能内化能力。

Abstract: Recent work on LLM agents is shifting from external capability elicitation to capability internalization, enabling agents to retain useful skills without retrieval at inference time. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising direction, but many existing methods typically supervise students by scoring actions along student-generated trajectories. Such supervision has two limitations: teacher preferences are not validated by environment outcomes, and action-level scores underuse information from student rollouts, teacher rollouts, and their behavioral relationship. We therefore advocate outcome-verified teacher supervision and comparative learning over teacher-student trajectories. Based on this view, we propose Outcome-Verified Comparative Self-Distillation (OVCSD). OVCSD organizes failed student rollouts into a prefix tree, adaptively invokes a skill-conditioned teacher from student-reached states, and retains only outcome-verified successful continuations. It then applies localized comparative learning at the first state-aligned divergence and distills the post-divergence teacher suffix to transfer completion behavior. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop across three model scales show that OVCSD consistently outperforms skill-free RL and existing self-distillation baselines, achieving up to 29.7 and 5.4 absolute success-rate gains over the strongest baselines on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while adding less than 3% privileged interaction during training.

</details>


### [172] [MARS-RA: Rank Aggregation for Credit Assignment via Multimodal Comparisons in Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27967)
*Dawei Wang,Di Zhao,Xinyuan Liu,Marci Chi Ma,Xiaoyang Liu,Chengming Zhou,Gary Ushaw,Richard Davison*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS-RA框架，将多智能体强化学习中的信用分配问题转化为排名聚合问题，利用大规模多模态模型生成的智能体间成对比较来估计相对贡献，并用于基于势能的奖励塑形，在动态智能体数量和延迟反馈的具身AI场景中表现出鲁棒性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协同强化学习中，信用分配在具身AI场景下尤为困难，因为反馈有限且延迟、活跃智能体数量动态变化。传统的绝对贡献估计方法对噪声和动态参与不鲁棒，亟需更可靠的信用分配机制。

Method: MARS-RA将信用分配重构为排名聚合问题，利用大规模多模态模型生成智能体间的成对比较（基于贡献），从而从绝对估计转向相对估计，增强对噪声和动态参与的鲁棒性；将比较结果转换为贡献分数后用于基于势能的奖励塑形，并提供Shapley值作为解释性参考。

Result: 理论上证明了框架的收敛性和鲁棒性，在多种不同类型且具有挑战性的任务上实验表明，MARS-RA能够有效引导智能体实现协同合作。

Conclusion: MARS-RA通过相对比较替代绝对估计的信用分配思路，结合大模型与势能奖励塑形，为动态多智能体协同场景提供了一种鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Credit assignment is a fundamental challenge in cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, particularly in embodied AI settings characterized by limited and delayed feedback as well as dynamically changing numbers of active agents. We propose MARS-RA, a framework that reformulates credit assignment as a rank aggregation problem using contribution-based pairwise comparisons among agents generated by large multimodal models. This shift from absolute to relative estimation ensures robustness against noise and dynamic agent participation, converting comparison results into contribution scores for potential-based reward shaping. We provide theoretical justification for the convergence and robustness of the proposed framework, and show that Shapley values can be used as an interpretive reference. Experimental results on challenging tasks of different types indicate that MARS-RA can guide agents toward effective cooperation.

</details>


### [173] [Share the Judge, Learn the Deferral: Where Specialization Helps LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27984)
*Weining Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过99,952条基于rubric的评测样本与RewardBench 2级联实验，对比"将领域专门化编码进评估器权重"与"编码进决定何时信任其判断的规则"两种架构，发现后者（即通过释放边界/延迟规则进行领域适配）更优，建议共享判断学习直到数据足够再分裂，并将领域适配放在可审计的释放边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统扩大了"生成候选输出"与"评审输出"之间的差距。作者提出一个实际的架构问题：领域专业化应当内置于评估器的权重中，还是内置于决定何时可以信任其判断的规则中？这关乎评估器的可扩展路径与可信度治理。

Method: 在99,952条公开的rubric条件化示例上进行对比实验：(1) 对比提供正确rubric vs 无关rubric vs 仅响应对照的准确率差异；(2) 将训练语料划分为八个criterion-family LoRA评估器，对比单一大rank-64适配器；(3) 从共享的已训练评估器初始化家族适配器；(4) 在RewardBench 2上，通过学习的正确性头部将示例路由到0.6B-4B-8B级联，不改变任何奖励分数，并跨20个锁定重分区执行精确单侧95%风险审计。

Result: 提供正确rubric比仅响应对照提高锁定测试准确率2.11分；替换为无关rubric则损失2.66分。八路criterion-family LoRA划分失去10.05分，审计覆盖率从24.44%降至5.43%。单一rank-64适配器不重现该损失。从共享评估器初始化可恢复至76.85%，比从头训练高19.94分。在RewardBench 2上，级联达到89.40%准确率（8B单独为84.75%），仅用0.415归一化参数计算；边界规则保持约84.8%准确率却至少用0.94计算量。

Conclusion: 建议的设计准则：在数据足够支撑分裂之前共享判断学习，将领域特定适配放置在可审计的释放边界中。即专门化应治理"何时推迟/信任"而非"如何判断"，这能同时提升准确率、降低计算开销并保障审计合规性。

Abstract: Agentic systems have widened the gap between producing candidate outputs and reviewing them. This paper asks a practical architectural question: should domain specialization be built into an evaluator's weights, or into the rule that decides when its judgment can be trusted? We study 99,952 public, rubric-conditioned examples. Supplying the correct rubric improves locked-test accuracy by 2.11 points over a response-only control; replacing it with an unrelated rubric costs 2.66 points. Dividing the same training corpus among eight criterion-family LoRA judges, however, loses 10.05 points and cuts audited coverage at a 5% risk target from 24.44% to 5.43%. Matching the bank's stored capacity with one rank-64 adapter does not reproduce this loss. Nor is the result explained by learning rate or optimizer steps. Initializing the family adapters from a shared, trained judge recovers test accuracy to 76.85%, 19.94 points above scratch training at the same learning rate (95% interval 18.88-21.02). The result changes when specialization governs deferral rather than judgment. On RewardBench 2, learned correctness heads route examples through a 0.6B-4B-8B cascade without changing any reward score. Across 20 locked repartitions, the cascade attains 89.40% accuracy, compared with 84.75% for 8B alone, at 0.415 normalized parameter compute. Every run passes an exact one-sided 95% risk audit; margin-based rules remain near 84.8% accuracy while using at least 0.94 compute. These results suggest a qualified design rule: share the learning of judgment until there is enough data to justify a split, and place domain-specific adaptation in an audited release boundary.

</details>


### [174] [SKIMIX: Multi-Agent Harness-Time Scaling with Skill Mixture for Dynamic Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27994)
*Jia Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SKIMIX多智能体框架，通过嵌入式技能检索、次模抗稀释路由和自适应技能进化实现智能体间协作。在六个推理基准上，该框架显著提升开放数学推理能力，但对选择题任务增益有限甚至为负，且智能体数量扩展呈非单调性，大部分改进来自第一轮精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体日益依赖大规模技能库，但如何选择、组合和维护这些技能仍然十分困难，现有方法缺乏系统性的协作机制来有效利用多样化技能组合。

Method: SKIMIX是一个多智能体框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)基于嵌入的技能检索，(2)次模抗稀释路由（submodular anti-dilution routing），(3)自适应技能进化。不同技能组合的智能体通过迭代精炼过程进行协作。

Result: 在六个推理基准上，多智能体协作显著改善了开放数学推理任务，但在选择题任务上增益有限甚至为负。智能体数量扩展呈非单调趋势，且大多数改进集中在第一轮精炼阶段。

Conclusion: 任务特性决定了技能级集成是否有效，该研究为可扩展智能体设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly rely on large skill libraries, but selecting, combining, and maintaining skills remains difficult. We propose SKIMIX, a multi-agent framework in which agents with different skill portfolios collaborate through iterative refinement. SKIMIX combines embedding-based skill retrieval, submodular anti-dilution routing, and adaptive skill evolution. Across six reasoning benchmarks, multi-agent collaboration substantially improves open-ended mathematical reasoning but offers limited or negative gains on multiple-choice tasks. Agent-count scaling is non-monotonic, and most improvements arise during the first refinement round. These results show that task characteristics determine whether skill-level ensembles help and provide practical guidance for scalable agent design.

</details>


### [175] [MMLDSum-LLM: Multimodal Long-Document Summarization with Visual-Alignment and Keyword-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28006)
*Xianpeng Zhang,Jiahua Yang,Dongyu Chen,Lei zhang,Jian Ma,Xu guohuan,Haonan Lu,Tianhuang Su,Chuangchuang Wang,Kai Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMLDSum-Bench（多模态长文档摘要基准）和MMLDSum-LLM（两阶段训练框架），用于解决多模态大语言模型在长文档摘要中的关键信息遗漏和跨模态幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态长文档是专业知识的核心载体，关键证据稀疏分布在段落和模态间，导致多模态大模型在摘要时出现关键信息遗漏和跨模态幻觉。其根本原因在于长距离依赖建模中的注意力漂移以及模态间对齐的差距。

Method: 1) 构建MMLDSum-Bench高质量基准，涵盖多个领域、上下文长度尺度和视觉-文本模态分布。2) 提出MMLDSum-LLM可复现的两阶段训练框架：第一阶段采用监督微调，结合视觉对齐加权损失和关键词感知加权损失；第二阶段采用GRPO，使用多目标奖励（关键词覆盖率、图文对齐、ROUGE和长度控制）。

Result: 在MMLDSum-Bench上，与领先闭源和开源多模态模型进行统一评估协议下的广泛对比实验（包括LLM-as-a-judge评分、原子声明精确率/召回率、图文对齐和ROUGE），结果表明该方法显著提升了关键信息覆盖率和跨模态一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的MMLDSum-LLM框架有效解决了多模态长文档摘要中的注意力漂移和模态对齐缺失问题，在关键信息覆盖和跨模态一致性方面均取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Multimodal long documents are core carriers of professional knowledge, where critical evidence is sparsely distributed across paragraphs and modalities. This easily causes key information omission and cross-modal hallucinations in summarization by multimodal LLMs. These issues stem from attention drift in long-range dependency modeling and gaps in inter-modal alignment. To address this, we introduce MMLDSum-Bench, a high-quality benchmark for multimodal long-document summarization, covering multiple domains, context-length scales, and visual-textual modality distributions. We further propose MMLDSum-LLM, a reproducible two-stage training framework that combines supervised fine-tuning with visual-alignment weighted loss and keyword-aware weighted loss, followed by GRPO with a multi-objective reward (keyword coverage, image-text alignment, ROUGE, and length control). Extensive experiments on MMLDSum-Bench, comparing against leading closed-source and open-source multimodal models under a unified evaluation protocol - including LLM-as-a-judge scoring, atomic-claim precision/recall, image-text alignment (ITA), and ROUGE - demonstrate that our approach significantly improves key-information coverage and cross-modal consistency.

</details>


### [176] [MUL-T: Decoding Spatial Cellular Architecture in Multiplexed Tissue Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28030)
*Farzaneh Seyedshahi,Kai Rakovic,Adalberto Claudio Quiros,John LeQuesne,Ke Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MUL-T，一个轻量级Transformer框架，将组织架构建模为离散细胞token上的掩码上下文预测任务。通过无任务特定监督学习上下文[CLS]嵌入，MUL-T捕获高阶细胞交互，在多种临床下游任务中优于经典特征基线，并以远少于基座ViT模型的参数和训练成本达到可比的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重成像（multiplexed imaging）数据的组织解析需要同时建模细胞表型及其空间上下文。现有方法依赖手工特征（如标记物强度统计或细胞类型比例），在异质标记物panel的队列中难以扩展和泛化。

Method: MUL-T采用轻量级Transformer框架，将组织架构重构为离散细胞token上的掩码上下文预测任务；通过该自监督预训练目标学习上下文化的[CLS]嵌入，无需任务特定监督。

Result: 在多个临床任务中（核心级肿瘤模式分类、患者级分级、PD-L1阳性预测、跨数据集治疗反应预测），MUL-T一致优于基于经典特征的方法，并以显著更少的参数和更低的训练成本达到与基座ViT模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: MUL-T验证了以掩码上下文预测方式学习空间细胞交互表征的有效性与高效性，为多重成像下组织架构分析提供了一种轻量且可泛化的替代方案。

Abstract: Understanding tissue organisation in multiplexed imaging requires modelling both cellular phenotypes and their spatial context. Existing approaches typically rely on handcrafted features, such as marker intensity statistics or cell-type proportions, which often fail to scale or generalise across cohorts with heterogeneous marker panels. We introduce MUL-T, a lightweight transformer framework that reframes tissue architecture as a masked contextual prediction task over discrete cell tokens. By learning contextualised [CLS] embeddings without task-specific supervision, the model captures higher-order cellular interactions while remaining computationally efficient. We evaluate MUL-T on several clinically relevant downstream tasks, including core-level tumour pattern classification, patient-level grading, PD-L1 positivity prediction, and cross-dataset treatment response prediction. Across tasks, MUL-T consistently outperforms classical feature-based baselines and achieves performance comparable to a foundation ViT model, despite substantially fewer parameters and lower training cost.

</details>


### [177] [DataClawEval: A Benchmark for Data Engineering Agents in Real Industrial Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28033)
*Debin Meng,Jiaming Yang,Zefang Zong,Tengyue Xu,Haining Xie,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataClawEval，首个专门评估LLM智能体在真实数据工程场景中端到端任务完成能力的基准，包含100个覆盖五种执行引擎的任务，采用确定性规则脚本在隔离沙箱中评分。评估16个前沿智能体后发现最强模型仅达74.9分，且各模型在不同引擎上各有专长，表明自主数据工程仍是未解决的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦于简化的Text-to-SQL翻译或数据分析，缺乏对端到端数据工程这一关键复杂领域的评估，因此需要构建一个专门评估LLM智能体端到端任务完成能力的综合基准。

Method: 基于企业专业数据工程师编写的生产级代码构建100个严格的端到端任务，覆盖PySpark、MySQL、HiveSQL、PrestoSQL/Trino和FlinkSQL五种执行引擎；每个任务在案例特定、隔离的沙箱中执行，并通过确定性、基于规则的脚本来评分，而非非确定性的LLM-as-a-judge方式。

Result: 评估16个前沿智能体后发现：最强模型总体得分仅为74.9，且没有单一模型占据主导地位——每个模型在不同引擎上各有优势，显示出严格的领域专精而非全能能力。

Conclusion: 自主数据工程仍然是一个艰巨且尚未解决的挑战，当前LLM智能体在该领域的表现存在显著局限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed to automate complex workflows, promising to revolutionize data management and processing. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on simplified Text-to-SQL translation or data analysis, leaving the critical and complex domain of end-to-end data engineering largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce DataClawEval, the first comprehensive benchmark designed specifically to evaluate the end-to-end task completion capabilities of autonomous agents in real-world data engineering scenarios. Built upon production-grade code authored by professional enterprise data engineers, it comprises 100 rigorous, end-to-end tasks spanning five execution engines: PySpark, MySQL, HiveSQL, PrestoSQL/Trino, and FlinkSQL. Rather than non-deterministic LLM-as-a-judge scoring, each task is executed within a case-specific, isolated sandbox and graded by deterministic, rule-based scripts. Evaluating 16 frontier agents exposes critical limitations: The strongest model attains only 74.9 overall, and no single model dominates, as each excels on a different engine, revealing strict domain specialization rather than omnipotent proficiency. Thus, autonomous data engineering remains a formidable, unresolved challenge. We release our dataset, containerized environments, and deterministic evaluation scripts at https://github.com/Dicemy/DataClawEval/tree/master

</details>


### [178] [SKILL-KD: Contrastive Skill Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28048)
*Qiming Shi,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yunfan Zhou,Di Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILL-KD是一个对比式技能蒸馏框架，将技能作为异构能力智能体之间的显式蒸馏媒介。它通过分析学生失败轨迹与教师轨迹之间的可操作差异，生成文本技能补丁，并通过重新运行学生来迭代评估和精炼补丁，同时利用Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation防止技能漂移。在五个智能体基准和两种学生设置下，SKILL-KD一致优于冻结学生智能体的固定模型适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能获取方法将技能视为经验总结、记忆条目或成功示范的直接摘要，这导致能力较弱的学生智能体无法有效利用技能——当学生因缺乏任务知识或操作策略而失败时，其失败轨迹缺乏推断缺失行为的足够证据，而教师轨迹又过于隐晦难以内化为可复用的指导。这种"能力鸿沟"需要更显式的蒸馏机制。

Method: SKILL-KD包含三个核心组件：(1) 对比式蒸馏：给定学生失败轨迹和教师成功轨迹，将两者的可操作差异蒸馏为文本技能补丁；(2) 迭代评估与精炼：通过重新运行学生来评估补丁有效性，失败时迭代修正；(3) Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation：维护轨迹关联的编辑历史，决定每个补丁应新增规则、删除/修改现有规则还是跳过，以防止局部更新累积导致技能漂移。

Result: 在五个智能体基准和两种学生设置（即不同能力层级的作为学生的LLM）下，SKILL-KD一致地提升了冻结学生智能体的表现，优于固定模型适应基线方法。

Conclusion: 将技能视为显式蒸馏媒介而非简单经验总结，能够有效弥合强弱智能体之间的能力差距；通过对比蒸馏、迭代评估和防漂移的规则整合机制，SKILL-KD为异构能力智能体间的知识传递提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Skill-based prompting has become a practical mechanism for improving large language model (LLM) agents, yet existing skill acquisition methods often treat skills as experience summaries, memory entries, or direct summaries of successful demonstrations. This creates a mismatch for weaker student agents: when a student fails because it lacks task knowledge or operational strategy, its failed trajectory may not contain enough evidence to infer the missing behavior, while the teacher trajectory may be too implicit to be internalized as reusable guidance. We propose SKILL-KD, a contrastive skill distillation framework that treats skills as an explicit distillation medium between agents of different capabilities. Given a student failure and the teacher trajectory on the same task, SKILL-KD distills their actionable discrepancy into a textual skill patch, evaluates the patch by re-running the student, and iteratively refines the patch when the student still fails. To prevent repeated local updates from causing skill drift, SKILL-KD further maintains trace-linked edit histories and performs Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation, deciding whether each patch should add a new rule, delete or modify an existing rule, or be skipped. Across five agent benchmarks and two student settings, SKILL-KD consistently improves frozen student agents over fixed-model adaptation baselines.

</details>


### [179] [IndustryForge-27B: A Domain-Enhanced Multimodal Foundation Model for Industrial CAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28050)
*Nianchen Deng,Jiaxin Ai,Tao Hu,Shu Zou,Yurui Dong,Siqi Li,Xinyu Cai,Xuemeng Yang,Licheng Wen,Hongbin Zhou,Hairong Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Botian Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IndustryForge-27B，一个基于Qwen3.5-VL-27B的工业CAD专用多模态大模型，通过整合六类工业CAD子语料（约52k多模态样本）进行统一多任务SFT训练，在四个CAD基准上平均提升33.65个百分点并全面超越GPT-5.4，同时在11个通用能力基准上保持甚至小幅提升基础模型性能（均值+1.56pp），无灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业CAD设计与制造对多模态基础模型有独特要求：需同时理解工程图纸和3D几何截图、编写参数化建模脚本和Windows COM API代码，并覆盖从单个零件到装配体的完整范围。通用多模态模型在这些专业任务上表现不足，而单任务微调过于狭窄，无法支持上层智能体的多样化调用需求。

Method: 作者在Qwen3.5-VL-27B基础上，策划并整合了六个工业CAD子语料库，总计约52k多模态样本，包括：CAD视觉问答（CAD-VQA）、参数化CAD代码（text2cadquery）、装配级CAD代码（text2cadquery-assembly），以及针对Inventor/SolidWorks的三个COM子语料（com_2d/com_3d/com_assembly），并使用统一的多任务SFT（监督微调）配方进行训练。

Result: 在四个CAD领域基准上，IndustryForge-27B相比基础模型平均提升33.65个百分点，并在全部四个基准上超越强闭源模型GPT-5.4；在11个通用能力基准上，模型保持并小幅提升基础模型性能（平均+1.56pp），未出现灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: IndustryForge-27B将作为下游工业智能体项目的通用基座，为覆盖从CAD设计到工业软件操作、从零件到装配体、从单次生成到闭环自改进的全栈工业智能体提供统一起点。

Abstract: Automating industrial CAD design and manufacturing places distinctive demands on multimodal foundation models: the model must see engineering drawings and 3D geometry screenshots, write correct parametric-modelling scripts and Windows COM API code, and cover the full range from single parts to assemblies. General-purpose multimodal models fall short on these tasks, while single-task fine-tuning is too narrow to support the diverse calls that upper-layer agents issue. We build IndustryForge-27B on top of Qwen3.5-VL-27B by curating and integrating six industrial-CAD sub-corpora totalling $\sim$52k multimodal samples---CAD Visual QA (CAD-VQA), parametric CAD code (text2cadquery), assembly-level CAD code (text2cadquery-assembly), and three COM sub-corpora for Inventor / SolidWorks (com_2d / com_3d / com_assembly)---and training with a unified multi-task SFT recipe. Across four CAD-domain benchmarks IndustryForge-27B lifts the base model by $+33.65$~pp on average and outperforms the strong closed-source model GPT-5.4 on all four; across eleven general-capability benchmarks it retains, and slightly improves upon, the base model ($+1.56$~pp mean, no catastrophic forgetting). IndustryForge-27B will serve as the common substrate for downstream industrial-agent projects, providing a unified starting point for a full-stack industrial agent that spans from CAD design to industrial-software operation, from parts to assemblies, and from single-shot generation to closed-loop self-improvement.

</details>


### [180] [SemPIC: Learning Semantic Position-Independent KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28069)
*Hui Xie,Peng Xiao,Yutong Deng\textsuperscript,Shuoran Dou,Jian Yang,Jinyang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemPIC，一种通过训练 LoRA 启用的 Writer 将文档 KVs 编译为可缓存表示的方法，解决了长上下文检索和代理工作负载中前缀缓存无法利用重复文档复用、位置无关缓存（PIC）不可靠的问题。SemPIC 在保留预训练解码器（Reader）不变的情况下，通过行为蒸馏离线编译逐层文档 KVs，并引入 KV 梯度检查点技术降低训练内存。在三个模型和四个任务上，将平均 micro-F1 从 KV Packet 的 0.53 提升到 0.60，接近 Full Recompute 的 0.62。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文检索和代理型工作负载会反复复用相同文档，但前缀缓存无法利用这种复用，而位置无关缓存（PIC）因独立编译的 KV 状态缺乏未来消费上下文而不可靠。诊断显示，已有的边界条件化（boundary-conditioned）基线虽能减少可复用块边界附近的注意力偏差，但仍存在内容内部和任务级别的残留偏差，因此需要直接改造文档表示本身。

Method: SemPIC 训练一个启用 LoRA 的 Writer，通过行为蒸馏（behavioral distillation）编译出原生的逐层文档 KVs，同时将预训练解码器保留为不变的 Reader。所有适配仅发生在离线缓存构建阶段，保持标准 KV 接口和缓存命中解码路径不变。此外引入 KV 梯度检查点（KV Gradient Checkpointing）以在缓存 KVs 上保持梯度完整的前提下降低峰值训练内存。

Result: 在三个模型和四个任务上，SemPIC 将平均 micro-F1 从 KV Packet 的 0.53 提升到 0.60，接近 Full Recompute 的 0.62，验证了通过离线编译文档表示来提升缓存质量的有效性。

Conclusion: 位置无关缓存的可靠性瓶颈在于独立编译的 KV 状态缺乏未来上下文，SemPIC 通过行为蒸馏训练 Writer 离线编译文档 KVs，在不改变解码路径的前提下显著提升了缓存命中质量，接近全量重计算的性能，是一种实用的长上下文缓存优化方案。

Abstract: Long-context retrieval and agentic workloads repeatedly reuse the same documents under changing instructions, histories, and document orders. Prefix caching cannot exploit this reuse, while position-independent caching (PIC) remains unreliable because independently compiled KV states lack the future context in which they will be consumed. Our diagnostics show that a learned boundary-conditioned baseline sharply reduces attention deviation near reusable-block boundaries but leaves interior and task-level residuals, motivating adaptation of the document representation itself. We present \emph{SemPIC}, which trains a LoRA-enabled Writer to compile native per-layer document KVs through behavioral distillation while retaining the pretrained decoder as an unchanged Reader. Adaptation is confined to offline cache construction, preserving the standard KV interface and cache-hit decoding path. We further introduce KV Gradient Checkpointing, which reduces peak training memory without severing gradients through cached KVs. Across three models and four tasks, SemPIC raises mean micro-F1 over KV Packet from 0.53 to 0.60, approaching Full Recompute at 0.62.

</details>


### [181] [Echoverse: Deep, Evolving Environments for Training Computer-Use Agents at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28074)
*Yash Pandya,Sahil Gupta,Sarthak Harne,Archana Yadav,Kavyansh Chourasia,Hussein Mozannar,Vibhav Vineet,Sara Abdali,Corby Rosset,Yash Lara,Ahmed Awadallah,Ece Kamar,Akshay Nambi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Echoverse，一个将规范编译为有状态应用程序的计算机使用代理训练环境生成框架，并引入协同进化循环，通过读取等级化回放同时修复环境和提供训练信号。使用12个环境训练的9B模型在14个评估集上从36.5%提升至67.1%，接近其教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理需要能在其上操作、破坏和重置的应用来训练，但最关键的应用程序受登录和状态限制，因此使用合成环境代替。现有批量生成环境的流水线已将瓶颈从环境数量转移到环境内部质量，关键在于三个属性：环境的行为深度、是否针对代理实际失败的交互、以及是否随模型一起改进。

Method: Echoverse框架包含两个核心部分：1) 将规范编译为有状态应用程序，其任务根据应用自身数据库进行评分；2) 协同进化循环，读取每个等级化回放两次——一次作为对环境、任务和验证器的修复，一次作为模型的训练信号。此外，将环境用作强化学习环境，结合接地验证器与密集逐级判断器的奖励函数。

Result: 在12个环境上训练的9B模型从36.5%提升至67.1%（14个评估集），与教授它的更大前沿模型仅差14个点。浅层环境使准确率降至基础模型以下（80.0→75.0），而深层环境提升至85.0和65.0；针对单一界面控件的钻取训练可迁移到未见组件系列和开放网络；修复单个环境使模型从16.2%提升至38.5%。在强化学习设置中，结合接地验证器与密集逐级判断器将保留分数从58.8%提升至68.0%。

Conclusion: 环境的行为深度、交互针对性和协同进化能力是影响计算机使用代理训练效果的关键因素。Echoverse验证了通过高质量合成环境加协同进化循环可显著提升小型模型的计算机使用能力，并发布了包含4个环境的基准（含应用、种子数据和接地评分器）。

Abstract: Computer-use agents learn from what their actions change, so training one needs applications it can act on, break and reset. The applications that matter most are login-gated and stateful, so synthetic environments stand in for them. Recent pipelines generate such environments in bulk, which moves the bottleneck from how many exist to what is inside each one. The returns, we find, come from three properties: how much behavioural depth an environment carries, whether it targets the interaction an agent actually fails, and whether it improves alongside the model. We present Echoverse, which compiles specifications into stateful applications whose tasks are graded against the application's own database, and a co-evolution loop that reads every graded rollout twice: as repairs to the environment, its tasks and its verifier, and as training signal for the model. Trained on twelve such environments, a 9B model improves from $36.5\%$ to $67.1\%$ across fourteen evaluation splits, within fourteen points of the much larger frontier model that taught it. We examine each property in turn. On the same domains, shallow environments push live-site accuracy below the base model ($80.0 \to 75.0$) while deep ones raise it ($80.0 \to 85.0$ and $48.0 \to 65.0$); drilling one interface control across many renderings transfers to held-out widget families and to the open web; and repairing a single environment lifts the model trained on it from $16.2\%$ to $38.5\%$. The same worlds serve as reinforcement-learning environments, where a reward combining the grounded verifier with a dense per-step judge raises held-out score from $58.8\%$ to $68.0\%$. We release four environments as a benchmark, with their applications, seed data and grounded graders. Code: https://aka.ms/echoverse

</details>


### [182] [Group-Reflective Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28076)
*Binbin Zheng,Zijun Xie,Guanqun Zhao,Enlei Gong,Xing Ma,Xiaoliang Fu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Group-Reflective Self-Distillation (GRSD)方法，通过策略自身验证轨迹的群体反思和停止梯度快照对比成功/失败轨迹，生成能力对齐且能区分结果的自蒸馏指导信号，用于增强RLVR训练大语言模型智能体的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽然对训练LLM智能体有效，但终止奖励仅提供粗粒度的轨迹级监督，使得成功行为、重复错误和偶然选择混在同一个结果信号中难以区分。现有智能体自蒸馏方法要么依赖外部检索的技能，要么依赖更强的模型从单条轨迹中提取技能，容易与当前经验不匹配、超出策略能力范围或过于路径特定。

Method: GRSD的核心包括：(1) 对每个提示，策略在on-policy组内对每条已验证轨迹进行反思；(2) 使用停止梯度快照对比成功与失败轨迹的反思结果，构建组级特权指导；(3) 在此指导条件下，自教师通过调制基于结果的优势函数来细化回合级信用分配，同时保持验证器确定的学方向。

Result: 在多个智能体环境和不同模型规模上的实验表明，GRSD一致优于竞争性基线，并在未见任务上具有更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GRSD通过从策略自身的验证回滚中提取能力对齐且结果可判别的指导信号，有效解决了RLVR中稀疏监督和外部技能不匹配的问题，提升了智能体训练效果与泛化性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is effective for training large language model agents. However, terminal rewards provide only coarse trajectory-level supervision, leaving successful behaviors, recurring mistakes, and incidental choices entangled in the same outcome signal. Existing agentic self-distillation methods enrich sparse supervision with natural-language skills, but skills retrieved externally or extracted from a single trajectory by stronger models may mismatch current experience, exceed the policy's capability, or remain path-specific. We propose Group-Reflective Self-Distillation (GRSD), which derives capability-aligned and outcome-discriminative guidance from the policy's own verified rollouts. For each prompt, the policy reflects on each verified trajectory in an on-policy group, and a stop-gradient snapshot contrasts the resulting reflections from successful and failed rollouts to construct group-level privileged guidance. Conditioned on this guidance, a self-teacher refines turn-level credit assignment by modulating outcome-based advantages while preserving the verifier-determined learning direction. Experiments across multiple agentic environments and model scales demonstrate that GRSD consistently outperforms competitive baselines and generalizes more effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [183] [Distilling Answer Set Programming Theories from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28086)
*Nelson Higuera Ruiz,Markus Hofmarcher,Claudiu Leoveanu-Condrei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究是否可以通过神经符号方法，让模型在固定代理框架和求解器在环的条件下，从零开始蒸馏出完整且正确的ASP（答案集编程）理论。作者在三个VQA基准（CLEVR、GQA、CLEVRER）上测试了九个不同规模的模型，发现四个前沿模型中有三个在CLEVR上达到100%准确率，在GQA上达到92.8%-98.8%，在CLEVRER上达到92.7%-95.3%，但GPT-5表现参差不齐。加入其他数据集的参考理论时，其他前沿模型变化在±3.4个百分点以内，但对GPT-5产生显著负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零开始编写ASP理论是困难且耗时的任务。作者希望探索是否能让模型在求解器在环的代理框架下自动蒸馏出完整、正确的ASP理论，从而减轻人工编写逻辑程序的负担，并研究模型规模与该任务难度之间的关系。

Method: 采用数据集无关的神经符号协议：模型以单个提示和空文件为起点，在1小时时间限制内推导出完整的ASP理论，求解器在环路中验证并反馈。选择了三个公开VQA基准（CLEVR、GQA、CLEVRER），测试了九个模型，涵盖四个前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5、DeepSeek V4 Pro）、两个中端模型（DeepSeek V4 Flash、gpt-oss-120b）和三个开源权重模型（qwen3.6-27b、gpt-oss-20b、qwen3.5-9b）。此外还进行了将其他数据集的手写参考理论加入的实验以研究迁移影响。

Result: 三个前沿模型（Sonnet、Opus、DeepSeek V4 Pro）在CLEVR上达到100%，在GQA上达到92.8%-98.8%，在CLEVRER上达到92.7%-95.3%。GPT-5在CLEVR上达到98.7%但在GQA上骤降至41.8%、在CLEVRER上为86.7%。加入其他数据集的手写参考理论对其他三个前沿模型影响很小（±3.4个百分点以内），但使GPT-5准确率下降3-19个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，具备足够规模的前沿模型可以在1小时内在固定代理框架下从零蒸馏出几乎完整的ASP理论，这表明神经符号方法在自动逻辑程序合成方面是可行的。然而模型之间存在显著差异，且外部参考理论的引入对部分模型会产生不利干扰。作者已发布代码、提示词和蒸馏出的理论供复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: Writing Answer Set Programming (ASP) theories from scratch is a difficult and time-consuming task. We take a neurosymbolic approach to study whether a model can distill complete and correct theories, given a fixed agent harness with the solver in the loop. The protocol is dataset-agnostic: with a single prompt and an empty file as the starting point the model is given a 1-hour time limit to derive a complete theory. We chose VQA as the application domain, three benchmarks (CLEVR, GQA, CLEVRER), as these are publicly available and non-trivial. In order to study the model scale required for solving this task we nine different models: four frontier (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, DeepSeek V4 Pro), two mid-tier (DeepSeek V4 Flash, gpt-oss-120b), and three open-weights (qwen3.6-27b, gpt-oss-20b, qwen3.5-9b). Three of four frontier models reach 100% on CLEVR and 92.8%-98.8% on GQA; on CLEVRER, Sonnet, Opus, DeepSeek V4 Pro score 92.7%-95.3%. GPT-5 reaches 98.7% on CLEVR but drops to 41.8% on GQA and to 86.7% on CLEVRER. Adding handwritten reference theories from other datasets moves the other three frontier models by at most +/-3.4 pp but reduces GPT-5's accuracy by 3-19 pp. We release the code, prompts, and theories distilled.

</details>


### [184] [Diversifying Personalized Research Ideation against AI-Induced Homogenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28087)
*Rui Xu,Yunke Wang,Linwei Tao,Wenjie Xuan,Yong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DivAlign，一个四阶段流水线，用于在保持与研究者个性化对齐的同时，降低AI研究方向生成中的社区级冗余（去同质化）。在95位AI研究者的基准上，DivAlign将平均成对相似度从0.331降至0.294，最近邻相似度从0.704降至0.608，同时保持99.9%的研究者方向匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助科研选题系统存在两个盲点：一是粗糙的研究者表征可能引发缺乏个性化根基的主流方向；二是独立推荐会使社区研究方向组合集中于高概率的重复主题，造成同质化。

Method: DivAlign是四阶段流水线：1) 提取细粒度研究者画像；2) 基于画像生成候选方向；3) 沿可执行性、可理解性和增长潜力三个对齐维度对候选进行评分；4) 在保留研究者局部方向的同时，减少社区投资组合中的冗余。

Result: 在由5个子领域的95位AI研究者构建的基准上，相比粗粒度单次生成，DivAlign将平均成对相似度从0.331降至0.294，最近邻相似度从0.704降至0.608；相比独立top-choice变体，最近邻相似度从0.663降至0.608，同时保留99.9%的研究者方向匹配分。

Conclusion: DivAlign有效解决了AI辅助研究选题中个性化对齐与社区多样化的权衡问题，在降低社区冗余的同时几乎无损维持研究者-方向匹配度，为科研方向推荐提供了一种兼顾个体与群体利益的去同质化方案。

Abstract: AI-assisted research ideation has emerged as a promising paradigm for accelerating scientific discovery, with systems now capable of generating research directions conditioned on papers, topics, or lightweight researcher contexts. Yet current systems largely optimize individual suggestions in isolation. This leaves two blind spots. First, coarse researcher representations may elicit mainstream directions that appear broadly feasible, but lack sufficient researcher-specific grounding. Second, independent recommendations can concentrate a community's portfolio around recurring high-probability themes. To address these blind spots, we propose DivAlign, a four-stage pipeline for alignment-preserving de-homogenization. DivAlign extracts fine-grained researcher profiles, generates profile-conditioned candidate directions, scores them along three alignment dimensions (Executability, Comprehensibility, and Growth Potential), and surfaces researcher-local directions while reducing redundancy across the community portfolio. On a benchmark we construct from 95 AI researchers across five subfields, DivAlign reduces community-level redundancy while preserving researcher-direction fit. Compared with coarse single-shot ideation, it lowers average pairwise similarity from 0.331 to 0.294 and nearest-neighbor similarity from 0.704 to 0.608. Compared with the independent top-choice variant, DivAlign reduces nearest-neighbor similarity from 0.663 to 0.608 while retaining 99.9% of the researcher-direction fit score. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ruixxxx/DivAlign.

</details>


### [185] [PerturbMap: Cross-Context Transfer of Single-Cell Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28090)
*Panpan Cui,Yiqi Liu,Wenhao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PerturbMap是一种预测单细胞扰动图谱中缺失目标上下文效应的新方法，通过将源上下文中已测量的同一种扰动的响应通过源到目标的岭专家传输，并结合目标局部的低秩基，按路由可靠性加权，在黑色素瘤队列上比基线提升4.1%的MSE，且接近集中式参照的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞扰动图谱很少能在所有细胞上下文中测量所有干预，查询扰动通常在一个或多个源上下文中有观测，但在需要其效应的目标上下文中缺失。忽略已测量的响应会浪费查询特异性的实验证据，而直接复制或弱校准跨上下文信号又可能传递错误的信号。需要一个能够有效利用源上下文信息来预测目标上下文缺失效应的方法。

Method: PerturbMap通过组合两个部分来预测缺失的目标上下文效应：(1) 目标局部的低秩基作为基础；(2) 通过成对训练扰动拟合的源到目标岭专家，将同一种扰动在源上下文中的已测量响应传输到目标上下文，形成接受的提案。每个提案的权重由验证锚点上估计的路由可靠性决定。

Result: 在Perturb-CITE-seq黑色素瘤队列上，PerturbMap比目标局部低秩基在完全效应MSE上提升4.1%，并优于FedAvg、零响应、原始复制、校准复制和身份洗牌仿射等对照方法。它距集中式token匹配池化参照（使用更强的训练接口）仅差2.82×10^-6 MSE。条件均值特异性诊断也显示相同趋势：同一目标的前10余弦检索从低秩基的74.5%提升到PerturbMap的80.5%。

Conclusion: PerturbMap通过可靠加权跨上下文提案传输，有效利用了源上下文中已测量的同种扰动响应，显著优于忽略或直接复制跨上下文信号的方法，性能接近集中式参照，证明了其弱接口下跨上下文扰动预测的可行性。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation atlases rarely measure every intervention in every cellular context: a query perturbation is often observed in one or more source contexts but missing in the recipient context where its effect is needed. Ignoring those measured responses discards query-specific experimental evidence, whereas copying or weakly calibrating them across contexts risks transferring the wrong signal. We propose PerturbMap, which predicts a missing recipient-context effect by combining a recipient-local low-rank base with accepted proposals that transport the same perturbation's measured source responses through source-to-recipient ridge experts fit on paired training perturbations, with proposal weights determined by route reliability estimated on validation anchors. On the Perturb-CITE-seq melanoma cohort, PerturbMap improves full-effect MSE by 4.1\% over a recipient-local low-rank base and achieves lower MSE than FedAvg, zero-response, raw-copy, calibrated-copy, and identity-shuffled affine controls. It remains within $2.82\times10^{-6}$ MSE of our centralized token-matched pooled reference, which uses a stronger training interface. A condition-mean specificity diagnostic shows the same direction: same-recipient top-10 counterpart retrieval by cosine increases from 74.5\% for the low-rank base to 80.5\% for PerturbMap.

</details>


### [186] [An Instrument to Evaluate Governance Proposals: AI Policy Analysis at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28094)
*Paulo Carvao,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Isabel Adler,Jeffrey Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于AI治理政策分析的多维属性框架，通过混合方法（专家定性反馈+计算文本分析）构建基于实证的政策属性评分标准，将政策目标量化呈现为可比较的可视化。框架不评判政策有效性或可取性，而是聚焦于相关性维度上的对齐程度，并明确暴露分析假设（属性选择、评分标准构建、权重方案），允许用户评估框架内置优先级与自身规范性立场是否一致。研究还对比了通用大模型与领域训练的评分校准模型在基于评分标准的政策分析中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI政策辩论常陷入二元对立立场，掩盖了底层权衡与规范性假设，缺乏系统性、透明的政策分析工具。现有评估方法往往预设结果或缺乏可解释性，难以支撑复杂且不断演进的监管环境中各种治理方案的比较与审慎评估。

Method: 混合方法框架：(1) 围绕多个政策属性构建分析结构，允许用户表达优先级与张力而不预设结果；(2) 结合领域专家定性洞察与计算文本分析来设计政策属性评分标准(rubrics)；(3) 将不同政策目标的相对权重量化，并通过比较性可视化支持跨政策对比；(4) 以领域训练的评分校准模型为基准，对商业LLM的评分标准政策分析能力进行评测。

Result: 框架通过实证的评分标准实现多维政策评估，能揭示不同政策属性间的权衡关系；混合方法赋予评分标准以经验基础与计算验证；领域训练的评分校准模型可作为比较各通用大模型的基准，量化了LLM在该任务上的表现差异。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种不预设立场、显式暴露分析假设的政策分析工具，具有管辖权无关性，适用于政策制定者、分析师和研究人员在复杂AI治理环境中进行系统性、透明、可比较的政策评估与自我反思。

Abstract: This paper introduces a policy analysis framework for systematic, transparent assessment of AI governance proposals in an evolving and contested regulatory landscape. AI policy debates often collapse into binary positions that obscure underlying tradeoffs and normative assumptions. The framework structures policy analysis around multiple policy attributes, allowing users to surface priorities and tensions without prescribing outcomes. We use a mixed-methods approach that integrates qualitative insights from subject matter experts with computational text analysis to inform the design of policy attribute rubrics. This quantifies the relative emphasis of different policy objectives and presents them through comparative visualizations that support interpretability and cross-policy comparison. The paper also examines the use of commercial LLMs for rubric-based policy analysis, benchmarking their outputs against a domain-trained rubric-calibrated model with explicitly defined analytical assumptions. Rather than assessing policy effectiveness or desirability, the framework focuses on relevance and alignment across attributes. By making analytical assumptions explicit, including attribute selection, rubric construction, and weighting schemes, the framework enables users to evaluate whether its embedded priorities align with the users' own normative commitments. The approach is jurisdiction-agnostic and intended to support policymakers, analysts, and researchers navigating complex AI governance environments.
  Contributions: (1) multidimensional policy assessment through empirically grounded rubrics that surface tradeoffs rather than resolving them; (2) a transparent hybrid methodology combining feedback from subject-matter experts with computational validation; and (3) use of domain-trained rubric-calibrated models as a benchmark for comparing different general-purpose large language models.

</details>


### [187] [SciDataSailor: Deep Scientific Data Exploring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28098)
*Jiyong Rao,Yicheng Qiu,Chi Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Runkai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的智能体任务范式——深度科学数据探索（Deep Scientific Data Exploration），并构建了SciDataSailor框架，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索合成工具交互轨迹，使LLM智能体能够在真实科学数据仓库中进行导航、解析异构文件、执行分析并得出基于观测的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据集通常以层次化仓库形式组织，包含异构且相互依赖的文件，人工检查、整合和分析不仅耗时而且高度依赖领域专业知识。尽管LLM智能体在规划、推理和工具使用方面取得了显著进展，但现有研究大多忽视了它们通过可执行环境与真实科学数据资产交互的能力。

Method: 引入深度科学数据探索（Deep Scientific Data Exploration）这一智能体任务范式。提出SciDataSailor框架，将轨迹合成建模为蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），并设计四种任务特定的机制：难度分层探索种子（difficulty-stratified exploration seeds）、双反馈首次探索紧迫性（dual-feedback first-play urgency）、层次化策略到工具动作生成（hierarchical strategy-to-tool action generation）以及熵引导的分支（entropy-guided branching）。利用该框架构建了用于监督微调的SciDataSailor-SFT-2K数据集和用于评估的SciDataSailor-Bench基准。

Result: 构建了包含627个元信息摘要任务和586个科学问答任务的SciDataSailor-Bench基准，覆盖生命科学、地球科学和物理科学共27个数据集。同时生成了SciDataSailor-SFT-2K用于监督微调。

Conclusion: 通过平衡广泛探索与针对性利用，SciDataSailor框架能够合成工具交互轨迹，使LLM智能体在真实科学数据仓库中有效导航并执行分析任务，为科学数据探索提供了新的任务范式和基准资源。

Abstract: Scientific datasets are commonly organized as hierarchical repositories containing heterogeneous and interdependent files, making their inspection, integration, and analysis labor-intensive and reliant on domain expertise. Although large language model (LLM) agents have advanced substantially in planning, reasoning, and tool use, existing research has largely overlooked their ability to interact with real scientific data assets through executable environments. We introduce Deep Scientific Data Exploration, an agentic task paradigm in which agents navigate repositories, interpret heterogeneous files and schemas, execute analyses, integrate cross-file evidence, and produce conclusions grounded in executed observations. To operationalize this paradigm, we present SciDataSailor, a framework for synthesizing tool-interactive trajectories by balancing broad exploration with targeted exploitation. SciDataSailor instantiates trajectory synthesis as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with four task-specific mechanisms: difficulty-stratified exploration seeds, dual-feedback first-play urgency, hierarchical strategy-to-tool action generation, and entropy-guided branching. Using this framework, we construct SciDataSailor-SFT-2K for supervised fine-tuning and SciDataSailor-Bench for evaluation, with the latter comprising 627 meta-information summarization tasks and 586 scientific question-answering tasks across 27 datasets spanning the life, earth, and physical sciences.

</details>


### [188] [MIND: Lightweight and Effective Memory Injection Defense for LLM Agents via Intent-Aware Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28103)
*Dongyi Liu,Haixing He,Xiaobao Wu,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND，一个面向记忆注入攻击的轻量级防御框架。MIND利用意图感知的信息瓶颈（IB）从初始用户意图和轮级行为中提取紧凑表征，过滤多轮上下文中的冗余和任务无关信息，再用轻量级检测器识别恶意记忆，从而在降低攻击成功率的同时保持任务准确性和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体依赖记忆增强，但容易受到记忆注入攻击：攻击者注入的毒化记忆会使智能体偏离用户初始意图，导致任务失败。现有防御方法要么计算成本高昂，要么在多轮上下文中存在信息冗余问题，亟需一种轻量且高效的防御方案。

Method: MIND的核心是：1) 初步分析发现良性轨迹和毒化轨迹在初始用户意图与后续行为之间呈现可区分的关系；2) 基于此观察，采用意图感知的信息瓶颈（IB）从初始意图和轮级行为中提取紧凑的意图-行为表征；3) IB保留意图相关的跨轮攻击信号，同时过滤任务无关和重复信息；4) 使用轻量级检测器从所得表征中识别恶意记忆，避免重复的LLM审计开销。

Result: 大量实验表明MIND在降低攻击成功率的同时保持了任务准确性和推理效率。在ReAct-StrategyQA上，MIND分别将平均ASR-r和ASR-a降低了55.4%和55.3%，同时在平均准确率和延迟上与未防御的智能体持平。

Conclusion: MIND是一种有效的轻量级记忆注入攻击防御框架，能够缓解多轮上下文中的信息冗余问题，避免重复LLM审计带来的高开销，在防御效果与任务性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM-based agents are vulnerable to memory injection attacks: Agents may retrieve poisoned memory from attackers, which diverts their behavior from initial user intent and finally causes task failure. However, existing defense mechanisms either incur high computational cost or suffer from information redundancy in multi-turn contexts. To address these challenges, we propose Memory Intent-Aware Neural Denoising(MIND), a lightweight defense framework for memory injection attack. Our preliminary analysis reveals that benign and poisoned trajectories exhibit distinguishable relationships between the initial user intent and subsequent behavior. Building on this observation, MIND employs an intent-aware Information Bottleneck(IB) to extract compact intent--behavior representations from the initial intent and turn-level behavior. The IB preserves intent-relevant cross-turn attack signals while filtering task-irrelevant and repetitive information, and a lightweight detector identifies malicious memories from the resulting representations. As such, MIND mitigates information redundancy in multi-turn contexts while avoiding the overhead of repeated LLM auditing. Extensive experiments show that MIND reduces attack success rates while preserving task accuracy and inference efficiency. Notably, on ReAct-StrategyQA, MIND reduces mean ASR-r and ASR-a by 55.4% and 55.3%, respectively, while matching the undefended agent in average accuracy and latency.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Rephrasing: Book-Level Organization Improves Synthetic Textbook Data for Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28109)
*Jiawen Tao,Miao Peng,Yaoming Li,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Wenhan Yu,Guoan Wang,Nuo Chen,Tong Yang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的合成教科书数据流水线，将检索到的语料内容组织成完整的书本级文档（686K本教科书、32B tokens、覆盖15000+学科）。通过多项受控对比实验证明，书本级组织（而非仅内容生成或局部重写）是提升预训练性能的关键因素，平均带来+1.09的改进，并显著优于内容匹配、长度匹配和重写条件等对照组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于合成教科书数据的工作主要将收益归因于生成内容本身或局部重写风格，而忽略了内容是否被组织成连贯的书本级文档这一结构因素。作者希望系统性地研究文档级组织对预训练数据质量的影响。

Method: 提出一个可扩展的合成流水线：(1) 从预训练语料中检索源材料；(2) 按主题聚类为单元；(3) 规划分层目录(TOC)；(4) 将基于源材料的章节组装成完整书籍（Full设置），生成686K本教科书。同时构建多个受控对比：Split条件（内容与token相同但每章节独立成文档）、RandomConcat（随机拼接不同书籍章节控制长度）、Rephrase（独立重写检索文档无组织结构），用于隔离文档打包、长度和组织结构等因素。

Result: 用合成的书本语料替代自然书籍进行中训练，平均下游性能提升+1.09。Full相比Split提升+1.02（证明文档打包的贡献），相比RandomConcat和Rephrase分别有显著优势（排除长度因素和组织结构的价值），在Llama3-8B上同样优于RandomConcat和自然书籍。

Conclusion: 书本级组织是合成预训练数据设计中独立且有效的重要维度。除了生成高质量内容外，将相关内容系统性地组织为连贯的书籍结构能进一步提升语言模型预训练性能，为合成预训练数据的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Synthetic textbook data has improved language model pre-training, but prior work largely treats the benefit as a property of generated content or local rewriting style. We study a different factor: whether related content is organized into coherent book-level documents. We contribute both a scalable synthesis pipeline and controlled evidence that this organization matters. The pipeline retrieves source material from a pre-training corpus, clusters it into topical units, plans hierarchical tables of contents, and assembles source-grounded sections into complete books (our Full setting), yielding 686K textbooks (32B tokens) across 15,000+ disciplines. Replacing natural books in a mid-training mix with this corpus improves downstream performance by +1.09 on average. Controlled comparisons then disentangle the relevant design factors. A content-matched Split condition holds generated text and tokens fixed but treats each section as an independent document; Full's +1.02 mean gain isolates document packaging. A length-matched RandomConcat control that joins sections from different books remains below Full, ruling out document length alone. A retrieval-pool-matched Rephrase condition independently rewrites individual retrieved documents under the same audience-by-style scheme, without clustering, TOC planning, or book assembly; Full's +1.17 gain demonstrates the value of structured synthesis. On Llama3-8B, Full likewise outperforms both RandomConcat and Natural Books, supporting book-level organization as a useful axis for synthetic pre-training data design.

</details>


### [190] [BlueprintRepair: Typed Local Edits for Failed Lean Proof Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28110)
*Ruslan Khrulev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueprintRepair，一个基于蓝图（依赖图）的LLM证明修复接口，通过10种带模式检查的局部操作让模型修改证明图；并构建BlueprintTrace基准（142个受控失败及其完整修复轨迹）。实验表明，在DeepSeek-V4-Flash和Qwen3.6-Flash上，类型化编辑与精确源码补丁、整模块重写解决几乎相同数量的局部失败，但类型化修复每个已解决状态成本最低（补丁1.30x，重写2.06x），且在较少的token预算内即可接近最终覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Lean证明系统逐渐将证明组织为蓝图（形式化语句的依赖图），但现有的修复方式（自由形式的源码补丁或模块重写）缺乏结构约束，效率低且容易偏离目标定理。作者希望引入一种受模式检查约束的局部编辑接口，使模型能以更可靠、更经济的方式修复蓝图。

Method: 设计BlueprintRepair接口：通过10种经schema检查的局部操作（如添加、删除、替换节点/边）修改证明蓝图；每个操作必须指明编辑目标节点，防止改变目标定理；Lean验证每次修改，且接受的修复必须声明其证明使用的所有蓝图引理。构建BlueprintTrace基准，含142个受控失败及其完整的接受/拒绝修复轨迹。在匹配的源反馈、模型和预算下，逐状态、逐接口对比三种接口：类型化编辑、精确源码补丁和完整模块重写，并在两种模型（DeepSeek-V4-Flash和Qwen3.6-Flash）上评估。

Result: DeepSeek-V4-Flash下三种接口解决几乎相同的本地化失败数；类型化修复每解决一个状态成本最低（补丁贵1.30x，重写贵2.06x），且在每任务10000个完成token内即可达到几乎全部最终覆盖率，而两种自由形式接口明显落后。Qwen3.6-Flash解决更少的状态，但仍保持类型化修复成本最低、在写证明类状态上更优，且重复了局部化模式。

Conclusion: BlueprintRepair证明类型化、结构感知的图编辑接口比起自由形式的补丁与重写在修复效率上更具优势：在不多损失解决能力的前提下显著降低成本并提升token利用效率；跨模型表现出鲁棒性。这提示在LLM证明修复中，受约束的局部结构操作比大范围重写更高效。

Abstract: LLM-based Lean proving systems increasingly organize a proof as a blueprint: a dependency graph of formal statements. We introduce BlueprintRepair, a repair interface that lets a model change this graph through ten schema-checked local operations. An operation names the node it edits, so the target theorem cannot be changed. Lean checks every applied change, and an accepted repair must declare every blueprint lemma its proof uses. We also construct BlueprintTrace, a benchmark of 142 controlled failures with complete accepted and rejected repair trajectories. We compare typed edits, exact source patches, and complete module rewrites under matched source, feedback, model, and budget, one episode per state and interface. With DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the three interfaces solve almost the same number of the benchmark's localized failures. Typed repair is the cheapest per solved state (patching is 1.30x as expensive, rewriting 2.06x), and within 10,000 completion tokens per task it reaches almost all of its final coverage, while both free-form interfaces are well behind. A second model, Qwen3.6-Flash, solves fewer states but keeps typed repair cheapest, puts it ahead on the proof-authoring states, and repeats the localized pattern.

</details>


### [191] [ConMem: Contribution-Aware Memory for Long-Horizon Manufacturing Inspection Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28126)
*Bingchen Liu,Yuanyuan Fang,Lei Liu,Guangyuan Dong,Xing Fu,Yuanyuan Gao,Shuyue Wei,Xin Li,Xiangtian Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ConMem——一种贡献感知的记忆框架，用于LLM辅助的钢铁设备长周期巡检。它将巡检日志按功能证据单元分段，通过Shapley值估计每个记忆单元对下游诊断的贡献，并在受限记忆预算下保留高价值证据。在真实数据集上达到76.0%的QA准确率，相较于朴素8K上下文LLM基线减少88.2%的输入token和86.6%的响应时间，并能跨三个巡检周期保留早期弱信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期钢铁设备巡检需要对多个重复巡检周期中积累的异构记录进行推理。现有RAG系统将历史日志视为静态语料库，不评估记录的诊断价值，导致早期风险无法被及时报告。作者旨在解决历史巡检日志中弱早期信号被忽略、记忆资源浪费的问题。

Method: ConMem采用三步流程：(1) 将巡检日志分段为功能证据单元（functional evidence units）；(2) 通过Shapley式估计（Shapley-style estimation）评估每个记忆单元对下游诊断的贡献值；(3) 在受限记忆预算下仅保留高价值证据。整体集成到人机协同（human-in-the-loop）的早期风险筛查系统中。

Result: 在真实数据集上，ConMem的QA准确率达到76.0%，超越最强的直接可比基线。相比朴素8K上下文LLM基线，平均输入token减少88.2%，响应时间减少86.6%。消融实验表明功能角色感知的分段和基于贡献的价值评估有助于优先识别弱退化信号；实际部署中ConMem跨三个巡检周期保留了弱早期信号，为现场检查人员提供早期密封磨损预警。

Conclusion: ConMem通过贡献感知的记忆管理有效解决了长周期巡检早期风险漏报的问题，既提升了诊断准确性又显著降低了计算开销，且在实践中证明能够跨周期保留并传递早期弱退化信号，为设备维护提供及时预警。

Abstract: Long-horizon steel-equipment inspection requires reasoning over heterogeneous records accumulated across repeated inspection cycles. Existing retrieval-augmented generation systems treat historical logs as a static corpus and retain records without estimating their diagnostic value, failing to report early risk. To this end, we propose ConMem, a contribution-aware memory framework for LLM-assisted equipment inspection, supporting a human-in-the-loop early-risk screening system. Specifically, our ConMem first segments inspection logs into functional evidence units, then estimates each memory unit's contribution to downstream diagnosis through a Shapley-style estimation, and finally retains high-value evidence under a constrained memory budget. In experiments, we evaluate ConMem on real-world dataset and ConMem achieves 76.0% QA accuracy, exceeding the strongest directly comparable baseline. Relative to the naive 8K-context LLM baselines, it reduces the average number of input tokens by 88.2% and response time by 86.6%. Ablation studies also show that the functional-role-aware segmentation and contribution-based valuation are helping prioritize weak degradation signals for targeted field inspection. Practical deployments further confirm that ConMem retains the weak early signal across three inspection cycles, providing an early-stage seal-wear alert targeted for on-site inspectors.

</details>


### [192] [AgenticASR: Refining Speech Recognition in Real-World Scenarios via an Agentic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28175)
*Zixuan Jiang,Binghao Qiang,Jiaying Chi,Yanqiao Zhu,Kai Yu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticSR（Agentic Speech Recognition）任务及其实现系统AgenticASR，通过ASR-Refiner架构在音频流式输入时持续修订已生成的文本，实现去除口误、解决自我纠正并保留说话者最终意图的"音频到整洁文本"转换。作者还构建了附带细粒度原子评分标准的中英双语基准AASR-Bench，实验表明AgenticASR在多个ASR前端上取得最高分数，并通过人工-AI一致性研究和消融实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR追求逐字转写的准确性，但逐字转写文本包含填充词、重复、误起句和自我纠正等不流畅内容，增加阅读负担、掩盖说话者最终意图，并将未解决或放弃的内容传播到下游任务。已有的spoken-to-written方法只能处理完整的音频或已完成的转录，无法在后续语音改变前文含义时修订已生成的文本。因此需要一种能随音频流持续更新并修订输出的方法。

Method: 将任务形式化为音频到整洁文本的AgenticSR任务。提出AgenticASR系统，采用ASR-Refiner架构：ASR前端负责初始识别，Refiner模块反复变换有界活动上下文（bounded active context），并在新音频到达时替换对应的输出片段。这种机制支持任意时长流的持续输出与修订。同时构建AASR-Bench双语基准，使用细粒度原子评分规则（atomic rubrics）进行评估。

Result: 在多个ASR前端（front ends）上，AgenticASR在AASR-Bench上均获得最高分数。人类-AI一致性研究表明基于rubric的评判与独立专家评估高度一致。消融实验表征了Refiner容量、上下文长度的影响，以及在线与离线推理之间的质量-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: AgenticASR被验证为一种实用的、在持续语音中保留说话者意图的整洁转录框架。代码、AASR-Bench基准和演示将公开发布。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has achieved substantial gains in transcription accuracy, yet verbatim transcription does not necessarily produce readily usable text. It retains fillers, repetitions, false starts, and self-corrections that increase reading effort, obscure the speaker's final intent, and propagate unresolved or abandoned content to downstream tasks. Existing spoken-to-written methods process completed audio or transcripts but cannot revise emitted text when later speech changes how preceding content should be interpreted. We therefore formulate Agentic Speech Recognition (AgenticSR), an audio-to-clean-text task that removes disfluencies, resolves self-corrections, and normalizes written form while preserving the speaker's final intent. AgenticASR implements this task through an ASR--Refiner architecture that repeatedly transforms a bounded active context and replaces its corresponding output span as audio arrives. This enables continual emission and revision over streams of arbitrary duration. We also introduce AASR-Bench, a bilingual benchmark with fine-grained atomic rubrics. Across multiple ASR front ends, AgenticASR attains the highest AASR-Bench scores among evaluated systems. A human--AI agreement study shows that rubric-based judgments align with independent expert assessments. Ablations characterize Refiner capacity, context length, and the quality--latency trade-off between online and offline inference. Together, these results establish AgenticASR as a practical framework for intent-preserving clean transcription during ongoing speech. Code, AASR-Bench, and a demo will be released at https://github.com/AnXMuy/AgenticASR.

</details>


### [193] [Old Tricks, New Models: How Simple Image Transformations Break Modern AI-based Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28187)
*Marco Alecci,Francesco Marchiori,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Jacques Klein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对三个商业多模态图像审核API进行了大规模黑盒评估，发现通过简单的、无需梯度或模型知识的图像变换（如色彩反转、灰度化），即可轻易绕过这些基于大模型的审核系统，导致不安全内容被误判为安全。研究指出，用基础模型API替代传统分类器并不能本身提供可靠的安全边界，应将其评估纳入现实变换场景，并作为分层审核管线的一部分部署而非独立安全过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的任务特定分类器在内容审核中覆盖有限、缺乏上下文理解，而基于大型基础模型的商业多模态审核API宣称提供更广泛和更强的能力。作者希望验证这种技术转变是否真正带来了更稳健的图像审核，即这些新型系统是否比传统方法更难以被规避。

Method: 对三个已建立的商业图像审核服务进行大规模黑盒评估，测试七种简单且模型无关的图像变换（无需梯度、替代模型或对目标系统的任何知识），跨多个提供商、数据集、危害类别、感知相似性约束和变换强度进行系统比较，分析不安全到安全的决策翻转情况。

Result: (1) 三个商业服务均可通过廉价且无需梯度的图像变换被绕过；(2) 即使是固定变换（如色彩反转和灰度化）也能在保持人类可识别内容的同时，诱导不安全到安全的决策改变；(3) 各服务在数据集和危害类别间的稳健性差异显著，其中多模态内容和自伤类内容存在明显弱点。

Conclusion: 用基础模型API替代传统审核分类器并不能本身提供可靠的安全边界。这类系统必须在现实变换场景下进行评估，并且只能作为分层审核管线的一个组件部署，而非作为独立的安全过滤器使用。

Abstract: While automated content-moderation systems have become essential for screening harmful content at scale, conventional task-specific classifiers often provide limited policy cov- erage and contextual understanding. Recently, commercial multimodal moderation APIs built on large foundation models have been introduced with the promise of providing broader and more capable safety filters. In this work, we analyze whether this shift also yields more robust image moderation. We conduct a large-scale black-box evaluation on three established commercial image-moderation services and compare their robustness. By evaluating seven simple, model-agnostic image transformations across multiple providers, datasets, harm categories, perceptual-similarity constraints, and transformation intensities, we find that: (1) all three commercial services can be bypassed using inexpensive image transformations that require no gradients, surrogate models, or knowledge of the target system; (2) even fixed transformations such as color inversion and grayscale conversion induce unsafe-to-safe decision changes while preserving content that remains recognizable to humans; (3) their robustness varies substantially across datasets and harm categories, with multimodal content and self-harm exhibiting pronounced vulnerabilities. This yields the conclusion that replacing conventional moderation classifiers with foundation-model-based APIs does not, by itself, provide a reliable security boundary. Such systems must be evaluated under realistic transformations and deployed as one component of a layered moderation pipeline rather than as standalone safety filters.

</details>


### [194] [Qwen-UI-Agent Technical Report: Toward Next-Generation Real-World Centric Foundation GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28227)
*Hanzhang Zhou,Panrong Tong,Xu Zhang,Quyu Kong,Chenglin Cai,Tianyu Xia,Gongjie Zhang,Jianan Zhang,Long Li,Long Chen,Lei Wang,Gaole Dai,Pengxiang Li,Liangyu Chen,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Qwen-UI-Agent 是一个面向真实世界的通用 GUI 基础智能体，覆盖移动、桌面、网页和深度搜索四大环境。它采用统一的动作空间，将 GUI 操作与 CLI 执行交织在一起，通过 AutoResearch 风格的数据飞轮和在线强化学习（支持超过100回合轨迹、超10000并发环境）进行训练，并配备轻量级 harness 层支持主动服务和跨端有状态工作流。在移动端基准上取得 SOTA（MobileWorld 82.1%、MobileWorld-Real 92.2%、AndroidDaily 97.5%），在桌面、浏览器和 GUI grounding 任务上（OSWorld-Verified 79.5%、WebArena 73.6%、ScreenSpot-Pro 81.5%）与前沿模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体有望成为现有数字设备的通用执行器，但当前面向真实世界的部署仍面临可靠运行、跨平台工作流、GUI 与 CLI 混合执行、长时程任务、主动服务和自主进化的多重挑战。作者围绕这些愿景，旨在构建一个真实世界中心化的基础 GUI 智能体。

Method: Qwen-UI-Agent 结合多样化沙箱环境与大规模真实设备移动运行时；采用统一动作空间，将 GUI 操作与 CLI 执行交织，并在单模型回合内生成批量动作；使用 AutoResearch 风格数据飞轮，由智能体自主构建任务与环境、诊断失败并规划后续迭代；应用在线 RL 支持超过100回合的长轨迹训练，利用超10000个并发环境加速数据收集；轻量级 harness 层支持主动服务启动和跨移动/桌面的有状态工作流。

Result: 移动端：MobileWorld 82.1%、MobileWorld-Real 92.2%、AndroidDaily 97.5%，达到 SOTA。桌面端：OSWorld-Verified 79.5%、OSWorld-v2 部分进度40.0%。浏览器端：WebArena 73.6%。GUI grounding：ScreenSpot-Pro 81.5%。在桌面和浏览器任务上与 Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.6 Sol 等前沿模型相比具备竞争力。

Conclusion: Qwen-UI-Agent 通过真实设备运行时、统一动作空间、数据飞轮与在线 RL 的组合，证明了在多环境（移动/桌面/网页/深度搜索）上实现高性能 GUI 智能体的可行性，尤其显著领先移动端基准，同时在其他任务上与前沿专有模型保持竞争力。

Abstract: GUI agents have the potential to become a general purpose executor over existing digital devices. To advance them toward real-world use, we envision agents that operate reliably on real devices, execute workflows across platforms, combine GUI interaction with CLI execution, complete long-horizon tasks, proactively initiate useful services, and autonomously improve their capabilities with minimal human effort. Guided by this vision, we present Qwen-UI-Agent, a real-world centric foundation GUI agent spanning mobile, computer-use, web, and DeepSearch environments. Qwen-UI-Agent combines diverse sandbox environments with a large-scale real-device mobile runtime. Its unified action space interleaves GUI operations with CLI execution and generates batched actions in a single model turn. An AutoResearch-style data flywheel uses agents to construct tasks and environments, diagnose failures, and plan subsequent iterations. Online RL supports training on trajectories exceeding 100 turns, with over 10,000 concurrent environments accelerating rollout. A lightweight harness layer supports proactive service initiation and stateful workflows across mobile and computer.
  Across a broad suite of evaluations, Qwen-UI-Agent sets state-of-the-art performance on mobile-use benchmarks while delivering competitive performance on computer- and browser-use tasks against frontier models, including Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.6 Sol. On mobile use, it achieves 82.1% on MobileWorld, 92.2% on MobileWorld-Real, and 97.5% on AndroidDaily. On computer use, it achieves 79.5% on OSWorld-Verified and a 40.0% partial-progress score on OSWorld-v2. On browser use and GUI grounding, it achieves 73.6% on WebArena and 81.5% on ScreenSpot-Pro, respectively.

</details>


### [195] [CDAE: Enhancing Perturbation Robustness in Pretrained Language Models with Contrastive Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28236)
*Sina Heydari,Amirreza Abbasi,Mohsen Hooshmand,Majid Ramezani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的对比去噪自编码器（CDAE），通过联合优化对比学习和重构目标来精炼预训练的BERT嵌入，使其对同义词替换、掩码和词丢弃等语义保持扰动具有不变性，从而提升句子表示的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型虽然显著提升了句子表示学习，但其嵌入对同义词替换、掩码和词丢弃等语义保持的文本扰动仍然敏感，这影响了表示的质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出轻量级的对比去噪自编码器（CDAE），通过联合优化对比学习目标和重构目标来精炼预训练的BERT嵌入，以学习对扰动不变的句子表示。

Result: 在多种扰动策略和不同强度下进行评估，与原始BERT嵌入和SimCSE相比，CDAE在扰动下始终能保持更高的嵌入相似度，且扰动越强改进越显著。

Conclusion: CDAE有效增强了句子表示的稳定性同时保留语义信息，表明扰动不变学习是提升句子嵌入质量的一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Pre-trained language models have significantly improved sentence representation learning, yet their embedding remain sensitive to semantic preserving textual perturbations such as synonym substitution, masking and word dropout. This work proposes a lightweight Contrastive Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE) that refines pre-trained BERT embedding by jointly optimizing contrastive and reconstruction objective to learn perturbation-invariant representation. We evaluate the proposed framework using multiple perturbation strategies with varying strengths and compare it against the original BERT embeddings and SimCSE. Experimental results show that CDAE consistently preserves higher embedding similarity under perturbations, with the improvements becoming more pronounced as framework effectively enhances representation stability while preserving semantic information, highlighting perturbation-invariant learning as a promising direction for improving sentence embeddings. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/ComputationIASBS/CDAE

</details>


### [196] [AI and Authenticity in Islamic Research: A Critical Evaluation of Generative AI Reliability, Hallucination, and Source Fidelity in Quranic, Hadith, and Fiqh Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28237)
*Muhammad Sajjad Akbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了六种主流生成式AI系统在伊斯兰知识领域的可靠性，通过50个开放式问题对《古兰经》解释、圣训、教法、伦理、牧灵建议等维度进行混合方法分析，发现当前AI可作为入门级伊斯兰学习的辅助工具，但不宜作为宗教裁决或学术研究的权威来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管穆斯林社群日益使用生成式AI获取宗教指导，但缺乏关于这些系统能否提供真实、可验证、可信赖的伊斯兰知识的实证证据。在高信任度的宗教语境中，需要严谨评估AI的可靠性。

Method: 采用混合方法框架，在澳大利亚和英国的真实条件下从参与者处收集数据，对六种生成式AI系统使用50个涵盖《古兰经》解释、圣训、教法学（Fiqh）、伦理、牧灵建议及教法学派（Madhhab）敏感话题的现实开放性伊斯兰问题，从领域准确性、引文验证、幻觉、教法一致性、不确定性处理、来源溯源和地域差异等维度进行分析。

Result: 研究发现当前生成式AI系统在伊斯兰知识领域存在幻觉、不完整引文、不可验证的宗教参考等问题，其教法分歧处理和不确定性应对能力有限，整体可靠性不足以支持宗教裁决和学术研究场景。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在伊斯兰知识领域作为入门学习和辅助工具有一定价值，但不应被视为宗教裁决或学术研究的权威来源，使用前必须与经认证的一手经典文献及合格学者专业知识进行交叉验证。该研究为研究者、教育者、AI开发者和穆斯林社群提供了首个综合性的实证评估依据。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly used by Muslims for religious guidance, Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith explanation, jurisprudential rulings, and Islamic education. Despite its growing adoption, there is limited empirical evidence on whether current AI systems provide authentic, verifiable, and trustworthy Islamic knowledge suitable for high-trust religious contexts. This study evaluates six leading generative AI systems using fifty realistic open-ended Islamic questions covering Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith, Fiqh, ethics, pastoral advice, and Madhhab-sensitive topics. Responses were collected under real-world conditions from participants in Australia and the United Kingdom and analysed using a mixed-method framework examining domain accuracy, citation verification, hallucinations, jurisprudential consistency, uncertainty handling, source provenance, and geographical variation.
  The study addresses four research questions: (1) How accurate and authentic are AI-generated responses across major Islamic knowledge domains? (2) To what extent do AI systems produce hallucinations, incomplete citations, or unverifiable religious references? (3) How consistently do models handle jurisprudential disagreement, Madhhab diversity, and uncertainty? (4) Are current AI systems sufficiently reliable for religious guidance, Islamic education, and scholarly research?
  Overall, current generative AI systems are valuable as assistive tools for introductory Islamic learning but should not be treated as authoritative sources for religious rulings or Islamic research without verification against authenticated primary sources and qualified scholarly expertise.
