### [326] [Encryption-Compatible Clustered Federated Learning via Distributed Expectation-Maximization over Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28338)
*Michael Ben Ali,Imen Megdiche,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAMECHE，一种基于元数据的分布式期望最大化（EM）聚类联邦学习方法，通过将服务器更新限制为加法操作，同时兼容安全FL加密方案，缓解了聚类联邦学习中隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率三者之间的权衡困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类联邦学习（CFL）方法在隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率之间存在此消彼长的权衡（即CFL三难困境）。基于元数据的聚类方法虽然高效，但与标准FL隐私保护机制不兼容，需要一种既能保持效率又能兼容安全加密方案的聚类方法。

Method: FLAMECHE将基于元数据的CFL重新表述为分布式期望最大化（EM）过程，将服务器的更新操作限制为加法运算，从而在不损失效率的前提下兼容实际的联邦学习安全加密方案。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种异构场景下的广泛实验表明，FLAMECHE提升了客户端模型的有效性，并实现了加密兼容的元数据聚类，优化了其在CFL三难困境中的定位。

Conclusion: FLAMECHE通过分布式EM框架和加法受限的服务器更新设计，成功实现了加密兼容的元数据聚类联邦学习，在CFL三难困境中取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) addresses data heterogeneity in federated settings by grouping clients with similar data distributions to enable effective training. Existing methods face a trade-off between privacy preservation, communication cost, and computational efficiency. We formalize this as the CFL trilemma, according to which improving two of these dimensions comes at the expense of the third. A prominent paradigm relies on metadata (i.e., low-dimensional representations of client datasets shared with the server) to enable communication- and computation-efficient clustering. However, such approaches are not compatible with standard FL privacy-preserving mechanisms. To address this limitation, we propose FLAMECHE, which reformulates metadata-based CFL as a distributed Expectation-Maximization (EM) procedure, restricting server updates to additive operations while preserving efficiency. This design enables compatibility with practical secure FL schemes. We conducted extensive experiments on multiple datasets under various heterogeneous scenarios. Results show that FLAMECHE improves the effectiveness of client models. It enables encryption-compatible metadata-based clustering, enhancing its positioning within the CFL trilemma.

</details>


### [327] [LEDGERMIND: Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28374)
*Enjun Du,Hange Zhou,Chenxu Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Ziyu Zheng,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerMind框架，将多模态智能体轨迹建模为受溯源约束的状态机，通过结构化证据账本作为轨迹状态、三层接地协议和自适应双路径调度器，解决最终答案准确率掩盖的四类失败模式（无依据中间推理、虚构引用实体、简单查询过度推理、修复放大），在多个多模态推理基准上同时提升答案准确率和轨迹忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视觉问答智能体的评估过度依赖最终答案准确率，该聚合信号无法区分正确答案是来源于有依据的证据、语言先验还是偶然的错误抵消。需要通过轨迹级别的忠实性评估和约束来揭示并解决这些被掩盖的问题。

Method: 将多模态智能体轨迹建模为溯源约束的状态机：1) 工具输出被归一化为结构化证据账本（Structured Evidence Ledger）作为轨迹状态；2) 下游推理和决策只能引用活跃账本条目；3) 在实体和数值层面检查接地（grounding）；4) 修复通过类型化状态转换实现，不能引入无工具溯源的内容。配套三大组件：三层接地协议（Three-Layer Grounding Protocol）、自适应双路径调度器（Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher）、事件触发验证修复引擎（Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair），后者具有形式上溯源非放大保证。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准和不同主干多模态大语言模型上进行的实验表明，LedgerMind同时提升了答案准确率和轨迹级忠实性，有效缓解了四类被最终准确率掩盖的失败模式。

Conclusion: 通过将轨迹状态显式建模为溯源约束的证据账本，并配套接地检查与修复机制，可以有效提升多模态智能体的轨迹级可靠性和可审计性，超越单一最终答案准确率的评估范式。

Abstract: Multimodal agents for visual question answering increasingly operate as multi-step trajectories that interleave perception, retrieval, and reasoning, yet evaluation still largely reduces to final-answer accuracy. This aggregate signal cannot tell whether a correct answer was reached through grounded evidence, language priors, or accidental error cancellation. We propose to treat a multimodal agent trajectory as a provenance-constrained state machine: tool outputs are normalized into a Structured Evidence Ledger that serves as the trajectory state, downstream reasoning and decision claims may cite only active ledger entries, grounding is checked at the entity and numeric level, and repair is realized as typed state transitions that cannot introduce content without tool-produced provenance. We instantiate this design as LedgerMind (Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger), augmented by a Three-Layer Grounding Protocol, an Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher that matches reasoning depth to question complexity, and an Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair engine with a formal provenance non-amplification guarantee. We use LedgerMind to target four recurring failure patterns that final-answer accuracy tends to obscure: unsupported intermediate reasoning, citation-backed entity hallucination (Phantom Grounding), over-reasoning on simple queries, and repair-time amplification. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks and backbone MLLMs show that LedgerMind improves both answer accuracy and trajectory-level faithfulness.

</details>


### [328] [Hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo for Order-Optimal Neural Actor-Critic in Average-Reward CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28390)
*Ankur Naskar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了层级化多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家方法，解决了平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中神经评论家估计的偏差-成本权衡问题，并基于此开发了原始-对偶自然演员-评论家算法，实现了T^{-1/2}阶的最优收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中，CMDP是强化学习的自然框架。虽然线性评论家的原始-对偶演员-评论家方法已被充分研究，但将阶最优收敛保证扩展到神经评论家（在平均奖励CMDP中）一直是未解决的问题。核心挑战在于神经评论家估计中的根本性偏差-成本权衡：在NTK分析下，降低评论家偏差会大幅增加评论家优化成本，阻碍了原始-对偶框架中的阶最优收敛。此外，现有的方法还需要提前知道底层混合时间，这在实际应用中是不可行的。

Method: 作者引入了一种层级化多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家，同时沿着轨迹采样和评论家优化两个维度进行去偏。该估计器仅需对数期望样本成本即可获得长时间评论家优化运行的偏差水平。在此基础上，开发了一个原始-对偶自然演员-评论家算法（primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm）。

Result: 所提出的算法在无限时域平均奖励CMDP中，同时实现了阶为O~(T^{-1/2})的最优性差距和约束违反。这是首次为具有一般策略参数化和神经评论家的无限时域平均奖励CMDP建立阶最优收敛保证，并且无需知道底层混合时间。结果在无约束设定下也是新颖的。

Conclusion: 本文通过MLMC神经评论家突破了神经评论家估计中的偏差-成本权衡瓶颈，建立了首个适用于一般策略参数化和神经评论家的平均奖励CMDP阶最优收敛保证，同时消除了对混合时间的依赖，推进了安全强化学习的理论基础。

Abstract: Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) provide a natural framework for reinforcement learning in safety-critical applications, where agents maximize long-term reward while satisfying long-term constraints. Although primal-dual actor-critic methods with linear critics are well understood, extending order-optimal convergence guarantees to neural critics in average-reward CMDPs has remained open. The main challenge is a fundamental bias-cost trade-off in neural critic estimation: under Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) analysis, reducing critic bias substantially increases critic optimization cost, preventing order-optimal convergence in the primal-dual framework. We resolve this bottleneck by introducing a hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) neural critic that performs debiasing simultaneously across trajectory sampling and critic optimization. The resulting estimator attains the bias of a long critic optimization run with only logarithmic expected sample cost. Building on this estimator, we develop a primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm that achieves both an optimality gap and a constraint violation of order $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$. This establishes the first order-optimal convergence guarantees for infinite-horizon average-reward CMDPs with general policy parameterization and neural critics, while eliminating the need to know the underlying mixing time. Our results are novel even in the unconstrained setting.

</details>


### [329] [Why Are GUI Agents Correct but Late? Decode on the Decision-Time Critical Path, Tested with Pre-Compiled Policy Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28399)
*Zihan Dong,Rui Qian,Qishi Zhan,Dongshen Peng,Kaixin Li,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AAPT（自适应预判策略树）通过在空闲屏幕期间利用冻结的多模态模型构建有界条件策略树，将决策过程从决策时间的关键路径中移除，从而解决计算机使用代理对瞬时GUI事件响应失败的问题。在对比试验中，AAPT将成功率从0.50提升到0.79（p=1.8×10⁻³），且不产生错误动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理经常在瞬时GUI事件上失败，因为它们在决策时间的关键路径上执行昂贵的自回归解码，导致正确的动作生成时，相关窗口已经关闭。现有方法（开环和预测-再规划基线）仍因在执行期间解码而全部失败。

Method: AAPT的核心方法是在空闲屏幕期间，使用同一冻结的多模态模型构建一个受约束的条件策略树，包含可观察的守卫条件、预授权动作和分支特定截止时间。树的规模被设计为覆盖模型自身的解码延迟。当事件发生时，一个轻量级观察器将变化的帧与预构建分支进行匹配，并立即执行对应动作，无需生成新文本。方法还涉及多个消融实验和预注册的oracle探针测试。

Result: 在成对试验中，AAPT将成功率从0.50提升到0.79（p=1.8×10⁻³），且不产生错误动作。效果在独立的多模态模型上复现（126对试验，p=4.9×10⁻¹³）。在外部基准上，AAPT整体性能与反应式基线相当，但两种方法表现出互补优势。消融实验识别出三个关键要求：快速观察器解码、有效的树规划和准确的分支路由。Oracle探针推翻了初始假设，指出分支路由是因果瓶颈。

Conclusion: AAPT在候选动作可以预先枚举的场景中表现最佳，而当候选动作无法预先枚举时，反应式执行仍然更强。该方法通过将决策延迟移出执行关键路径，在不修改底层模型的情况下显著提升了瞬时GUI事件处理的成功率。

Abstract: Computer-use agents often fail on transient GUI events because they produce the correct action only after the relevant window has already closed. We identify the main cause as expensive autoregressive decoding on the decision-time critical path. We propose Adaptive Anticipatory Policy Trees (AAPT), which eliminates this delay without modifying the underlying model. During idle screen periods, the same frozen multimodal model constructs a bounded conditional policy tree with observable guards, pre-authorized actions, and branch-specific deadlines. The tree is sized to cover the model's own decoding latency. When an event occurs, a lightweight observer matches change-gated frames to a prepared branch and immediately executes the corresponding action without generating new text. In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [330] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究交互式系统中大规模动作空间下的策略学习，涵盖在策略学习（上下文bandits、最小化遗憾）和离策略学习（利用日志数据）。提出结构化贝叶斯方法（meTS、dTS）用于在策略学习，以及结构化直接方法（sDM）、可优化的凹目标函数和基于指数平滑与PAC-Bayes界的悲观方法用于离策略学习，以应对探索效率低、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重高方差、外推偏差和优化困难等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模动作空间下的上下文bandits在在策略和离策略学习中均面临重大挑战：探索效率低下、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重高方差、外推偏差以及优化景观困难。

Method: 论文提出两大方向的方法：在策略方面，提出meTS（混合效应Thompson sampling扩展）和dTS（利用扩散启发先验建模动作间依赖）；离策略方面，提出sDM（基于潜变量的结构化直接方法），证明大动作空间中优化误差可主导估计误差，引入凹的、可高效优化的策略加权对数似然目标，并开发基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayes界的可微悲观方法来控制正则化重要性采样估计器的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 所提出的方法在动作间共享信息，遗憾保证依赖于有效动作数；在离策略学习中证明了优化误差在大动作空间中的主导性，并提供了可高效优化的凹目标和可控偏差-方差权衡的悲观估计方法。

Conclusion: 论文系统性地解决了大规模动作空间下策略学习的两大范式（在策略与离策略）中的关键挑战，通过结构化建模和信息共享、优化的目标函数设计以及可微悲观方法，提供了理论保障和实用的算法方案。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [331] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAdapt是一个噪声自适应的神经预解码框架，用于表面码量子纠错。它捕获综合征数据中的局部时空相关性，顺序适应演化中的噪声条件并缓解灾难性遗忘，将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器。在110种合成分布外噪声配置下持续降低逻辑错误率，在Google Willow基准数据上实现最高5.79%的逻辑错误率降低和9.32%的后端解码延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算中，性能不仅受物理噪声限制，还受经典解码器处理快速生成综合征数据的延迟限制。硬件噪声具有强、异质、非平稳的特点，且模拟到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定神经解码器的性能，亟需能够自适应演化噪声条件的解码方案。

Method: QAdapt框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 捕获综合征数据中局部时空相关性的神经预解码网络；(2) 顺序适应演化噪声条件的同时缓解灾难性遗忘的机制；(3) 将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器的接口设计。

Result: 在110种合成分布外噪声配置的旋转表面码存储电路中，QAdapt相比神经预解码基线持续降低逻辑错误率。在Google Willow基准数据上无需目标域微调即实现最高5.79%的逻辑错误率降低和9.32%的后端解码延迟降低。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且与解码器兼容的方法，能够在演化的硬件噪声条件下提升量子纠错的鲁棒性和后端解码效率，为其实际部署提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [332] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kohn-Sham谱嵌入（KSSE）方法，用基于稀疏图谱嵌入的能量模型替代密集CNN分类器。该方法将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图上，构造Kohn-Sham哈密顿量正则化拉普拉斯算子，在O(N log N + k²_mode N)时间内通过FFT求解D个独立通道谱问题。在ImageNet-1000上使用冻结的EfficientNet-B4特征，KSSE达到88.93% Top-1精度，仅需约21.24M参数，相较Swin-L和ViT-H/14分别减小10倍和30倍模型体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集CNN分类器参数量庞大且计算开销高，而物理启发的能量模型有望以更稀疏、更高效的方式实现图像分类。作者受凝聚态物理启发，试图将Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论与图神经网络结合，实现高精度、低计算复杂度的分类模型。

Method: KSSE方法包含以下核心组件：(1)将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图，构造正则化拉普拉斯作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量；(2)利用FFT在循环块上求解谱问题（借助Z/pZ的Pontryagin自对偶），复杂度为O(N log N + k²_mode N)；(3)通过星域手术（star-domain surgery）优化图拓扑，围绕码字构建局部凸性同时将残余受阻限制在ρ(B_γ)≤1+δ；(4)使用多尺度分形分析和分形学习率景观来认证从粗糙区（D2>3）到星域盆地（D2<1）的景观转变；(5)证明六项理论结果，包括广义Ihara-Bass恒等式、陷阱集特征值对应、加性通道可分性、手术定理、拟平稳扰动界和不动点收敛定理。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000转导协议下，使用冻结EfficientNet-B4特征（D=1792），KSSE达到88.93% Top-1精度，使用约21.24M参数。性能超过Swin-L（197M参数，86.4-87.3%）并与ViT-H/14（632M参数，88.0-89.5%）相当，模型体积分别减小10倍和30倍。

Conclusion: KSSE展示了将凝聚态物理（Kohn-Sham理论）与图模型结合构建高效分类器的可行性，大幅减少了模型参数量同时保持甚至超越SOTA性能，为物理启发的机器学习方法提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [333] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"短时图记忆"（short-term graph memory）的即插即用模块，在不改变生成器架构和原生更新规则的前提下，通过学习已评估分子来对候选池进行预筛选，从而在有限oracle预算下优先评估预测效用更高的分子。在标准分子优化基准上，该模块在不增加oracle调用次数的前提下提升了平均top-10分数，且在1000次调用的紧预算下对四种生成器均有增益。文章还分析了代理引导选择与不同生成器探索-利用行为的交互机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常在有限的oracle预算下进行，因此决定评估哪些分子与决定生成哪些分子同样重要。现有的生成器往往只关注生成策略本身，而忽略了oracle预算分配的优化问题。作者旨在利用已评估分子信息来引导后续oracle查询，使有限的评估预算花在更有价值的候选分子上。

Method: 作者引入"短时图记忆"模块，该模块包含一个在线图神经网络代理模型，用于在每个迭代轮次中预筛选生成器的候选分子池，仅将oracle预算分配给预测效用更高的分子。该模块是即插即用的，不改变生成器的架构和原生更新规则。

Result: 在标准分子优化基准上，基于片段生成器（fragment-based generator）的测试中，该模块在不增加oracle成本的条件下提升了平均top-10分数，且在任何oracle上均未低于基线。在1000次调用的紧预算下，四种生成器均获得了增益。分析表明，该方法的收益与生成器的搜索广度及其原生搜索对oracle反馈的利用效率有关。

Conclusion: 作者提供了一种简单有效的策略，可以在固定oracle预算下更精准地分配评估资源，并揭示了哪些类型的生成器能从这种预筛选中获益，为有限预算下的分子优化提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [334] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于思维链推理的安全运营中心（SOC）告警分流分类器，结合自动化提示优化、自训练和可验证奖励的强化学习训练，并引入单独的校准器读取完整推理轨迹以估计判断正确的概率。系统达到82.6%测试准确率，在高置信度操作点上良性/恶意召回率分别提升43.0%和18.3%，并证明训练后的校准器不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临告警疲劳问题——检测数量远超分析师可处理的能力。现有方法直接提示或微调LLM输出分流标签，但未训练模型推理检测是否为真实威胁，导致分类质量不足。

Method: 在真实且带人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上训练一个启用思维链推理的分流分类器，通过组合自动化提示优化、自训练以及带可验证奖励的强化学习进行训练。同时，由于CoT推理会降低标签token概率，单独训练一个校准器读取完整推理轨迹以估计判断正确的概率。

Result: 系统达到82.6%测试准确率。在高置信度操作点上，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%。训练后的校准器是必需的——未训练的置信度判断器会使高置信度召回率降至零。此外，微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型，表明针对性训练优于单纯扩大模型规模。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了针对安全告警分流的定向训练优于依赖大规模通用模型。CoT推理结合校准机制显著提升了高置信度分流性能，为解决SOC告警疲劳提供了有效方案。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [335] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了"阶段重放诊断"的核心假设——即从重建的token前缀续写等价于从原始解码器状态续写。通过在Qwen2.5派生系统上对200个项目进行匹配实验，发现在BF16精度下重建与保留缓存存在166处后缀和20处正确性标签的差异，但精度仅差1点；FP32下无解码分歧（95% Wilson上限1.88%）。通过对全部48层K/V缓存的双向移植实验，证明边界K/V缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度则调节其行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶段重放诊断是一种常用于分析推理阶段的方法，它假设从重建的中间token前缀继续生成与从原本到达该前缀的解码器状态继续生成是等价的。然而这一核心假设从未被严格审计过。作者希望在真实的推理阶段边界上检验该假设的有效性，特别是在数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）影响下，重建缓存与保留缓存是否真的产生一致的行为。

Method: 作者在Qwen2.5派生系统上设计了三组实验：(1) 200项匹配实验，在BF16下比较保留的实时缓存与一次性预填充的相同整数token重建，并在两侧放置精确副本；(2) 固定前缀2x2设计，交叉精度（BF16/FP32）与构建方式（保留/重建）；(3) 可行性桥梁实验将token级增量缓存与保留实时缓存逐token对齐，随后对所有48层K/V缓存进行双向移植实验——在多个检查点上验证发散续写是否完全跟随缓存供体。

Result: BF16下重建与保留缓存在166个后缀和20个正确性标签上存在差异，但准确率仅差1点（配对95%CI [-3.5, +5.5]）；FP32下无任何解码分歧（95% Wilson上限1.88%）。可行性桥使12/12行token逐位精确；保存账本审计复现所有保留轨迹。双向移植在24/24（主检查点）和43/43（后期检查点，结果盲）上使发散续写完全跟随缓存供体。

Conclusion: 精确token重放可以可重复，但不等同于保留实时状态的保真度。在测试的状态上，边界K/V缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体——移植K/V缓存就能移植行为；而数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）是调节该行为是否表现出来的关键因素。实际区分二者的差异来源可能主要是精度导致的浮点累积误差，而非缓存内容本身的信息丢失。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [336] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果行为绩效（causal performative）框架来分析算法补救（algorithmic recourse）中的博弈行为，揭示了忽略因果结构的补救策略会引发与真实资质不符的策略性行为、降低预测精度，而基于因果结构的补救策略能收敛到稳定均衡并减少博弈激励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法补救方法仅关注翻转模型预测，未考虑推荐改变是否真正提升个体资质或只是允许策略性博弈分类器，导致部署的补救策略引发行为响应、降低预测精度，并在模型重训练后失效。

Method: 通过结构因果模型（SCM）建模补救行动如何在特征间传播及其对真实标签的影响；将因果响应转化为非凸优化问题，并刻画可高效求解的稳定性条件；提出因果补救方法与标准经验风险最小化的对比实验。

Result: 忽略因果结构的补救策略会引发大规模、错位的行为响应；因果补救策略能达到稳定均衡并减少博弈激励。在半合成与真实信贷数据集上，该方法一致优于标准经验风险最小化，并减少因策略性行为导致的反复模型重训练需求。

Conclusion: 因果感知的补救策略是构建可持续、抗博弈的分类系统的重要方向，能够在提升个体真实资质的同时降低系统退化的风险。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [337] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了同图上的节点分类与链接预测跨任务迁移问题，提出无泄漏的评估协议，发现NC→LP在同质图上始终有益，而LP→NC仅在结构主导场景下有益，并引入CoTask Score（CTS）来指导联合任务下的机制选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的图数据常需在同一底层结构上同时支持多个预测任务（如节点分类和链接预测），复用监督信号有潜在价值。然而现有评估因不兼容的数据划分、不同的观测图假设和负采样规则等问题，导致关于同图跨任务迁移的结论不可靠。因此需要形式化该问题并提供无泄漏的评估协议。

Method: 作者形式化了同图NC-LP迁移问题，提出一个无泄漏协议：固定节点和边的划分，使用排除被评估边的共享消息传递图，并采用固定负样本进行链接预测。在三种骨干网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS）上系统评估双向迁移效果，并引入CoTask Score（CTS）以量化共享编码器同时服务NC和LP时的联合效用。

Result: 实验发现迁移具有强烈的方向性和可预测性：NC→LP在同质图上一致有益；LP→NC在朴素表示复用下是脆弱的甚至可能降低精度；LP→NC仅在结构主导区域（LP容易但NC未饱和）才可靠正向，此时LP起到结构预训练的作用。此外，简单数据集统计量（尤其是同质性）可以指导机制选择并避免负迁移。

Conclusion: 同图NC-LP迁移不是对称的，其方向和有效性取决于图的结构特性（如同质性）和任务饱和程度。CTS作为统一的联合效用指标，配合数据集统计量可作为实践指导，帮助研究者选择正确的迁移机制避免负迁移。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [338] [APO: Unsupervised Atomic Policy Optimization for 3D Structure Prediction of Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28553)
*Shentong Mo,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为APO（Atomic Policy Optimization）的完全无监督对齐框架，用于3D原子系统结构预测，通过双奖励机制（潜在结构模态强化和热力学稳定性约束）替代昂贵的实验标签，在晶体和抗体结构预测任务上超越全监督基线，同时提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配模型（如FlowDPO）在3D原子系统结构预测中依赖监督偏好学习与真实坐标对齐，但获取新颖晶体相或全新蛋白质的实验标签成本极高，形成了数据稀缺场景下的瓶颈。因此需要一种无需参考结构的无监督对齐方案。

Method: 提出Atomic Policy Optimization（APO）框架，将组相对策略优化（GRPO）适配到3D原子环境。核心包含双奖励机制：（1）通过样本相似度的特征值分解来强化策略的主导潜在结构模态；（2）通过热力学稳定性约束确保生成结构物理合理。框架在采样组内识别物理可行构型，实现模型的"自校正"。

Result: 在晶体和抗体结构预测的广泛基准测试中，APO一致超越全监督基线，在匹配率和结构保真度上达到新SOTA。此外，APO有效拉直概率路径，显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: APO表明内在物理一致性可以作为一种优于噪声监督坐标匹配的对齐指导，为数据稀缺场景下的3D结构建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Predicting the 3D structures of atomic systems is fundamental to advancing material science and drug discovery. While flow-matching models (, FlowDPO) have recently shown promise in this domain, their performance relies heavily on alignment with ground-truth coordinates via supervised preference learning. However, obtaining experimental labels for novel crystal phases or de novo proteins is prohibitively expensive, creating a bottleneck for structural modeling in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we propose (Atomic Policy Optimization), a fully unsupervised alignment framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth reference structures. APO adapts group-relative policy optimization to 3D atomic environments, utilizing a novel dual-reward mechanism: (i) a that reinforces the policy's dominant latent structural modes through eigen-decomposition of sample similarities, and (ii) a that enforces thermodynamic stability. Our framework enables the model to ``self-correct'' by identifying physically plausible configurations within sampled groups. Extensive benchmarks on crystal and antibody structure prediction demonstrate that APO consistently outperforms fully supervised baselines, achieving a new state-of-the-art in match rates and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we show that APO effectively straightens probability paths, significantly improving inference efficiency. Our results suggest that intrinsic physical consistency can serve as a superior guide for alignment compared to noisy, supervised coordinate matching.

</details>


### [339] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD，将vanilla OPSD（β=1的特例）推广为具有可控正则化参数β的通用策略优化框架，通过将RL目标闭式解转化为蒸馏目标（混合参考策略与教师模型的token级logits），以廉价蒸馏近似昂贵的策略优化，在数学推理基准上持续优于vanilla OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPSD在实践中的可靠性难以保证，需要大量工程调优。本文识别出这一困难的结构性根源：vanilla OPSD恰好是更广泛策略优化家族中β=1的特例，而β本应是一个可调的正则化参数而非固定值。

Method: 1) 将OPSD形式化为β加权的KL惩罚策略优化家族，β-OPSD的最优策略是参考策略与特权教师之间的几何插值；2) 不去直接优化高方差、昂贵的RL目标，而是利用其闭式解构造蒸馏目标；3) 每个β对应参考到教师路径上的一个目标点，通过token级logits混合高效实现；4) 使用Return-to-go（RTG）信度分配使token更新对齐序列级目标，同时保持OPSD的简洁性。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，β-OPSD在优化稳定性和下游推理性能上均持续优于vanilla OPSD。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一条从自蒸馏到策略优化再回归自蒸馏的原则性路径，在保持OPSD实用效率的同时，通过可调β参数提升了性能与稳定性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [340] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KAISEN是一个五阶段审计流水线，在合成基准上被测试至失败点，揭示了显著性追踪、阈值优化、机制诊断和漂移监控各组件的失效条件，表明审计组件在不同条件下可信度差异巨大，报告方差而非平均值是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在总体表现优异的背后，往往在不同患者亚组间存在实质性误差率差异。虽然已有审计流水线用于捕捉此类问题，但其各组件的可靠性从未被系统压力测试，导致不清楚哪些审计环节可被信任以及在何种条件下可信。

Method: 提出KAISEN五阶段审计流水线，包含亚组分层、差异度量、机制诊断、事后缓解和漂移监测。在包含16种疾病任务、15个来自Healthy People 2030的社会决定因素轴以及三个预先指定交叉项的合成基准上，评估至失败点（evaluated to the point of failure）。

Result: (i) 显著性计数与标准化EOD的秩相关系数从0.56提升至0.78，表明显著性追踪受各轴最小可检测效应影响；(ii) 分组阈值优化在48/48运行中降低EOD（均值差-0.285），而分组Platt缩放只有19/48运行改善，均值效应接近零，审计应报告方差而非平均；(iii) 机制诊断在144/144受控案例中分类正确，但在代理误设下48个模型驱动案例全部失败且无失败信号；(iv) CUSUM故障和误报受队列实现影响远大于疾病本身，参考阈值下27个误报和8个漏检中7个来自不同种子（卡方p=0.002），阈值不可跨队列迁移。

Conclusion: 审计流水线的不同组件具有截然不同的可靠性边界：某些组件（如阈值优化）稳健有效，而另一些（如机制诊断在代理误设下、Platt缩放的EOD效果、CUSUM阈值迁移）在特定条件下完全失效且无预警信号。审计应该报告组件行为的关键方差而非平均值。所有结果均为合成数据并已知真实标签，不构成临床有效性验证。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [341] [It's Not Just More Demos: Counterfactual Action Sensitivity Coverage for Data-Efficient Robust Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27261)
*Giovanni D'urso,Kaushik Roy,Nicholas Lawrance,Brendan Tidd*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CFNBC框架，通过测量策略在"干扰观测"下的动作漂移来选择针对性修复数据，以最少量数据大幅提升模仿学习策略对视觉扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动模仿学习策略对任务保持型视觉变化（如光照、干扰物、颜色变化）非常脆弱，即使微小扰动也会导致性能大幅下降。虽然增加数据多样性可以提高鲁棒性，但缺乏标准来判断哪些额外演示数据对特定训练策略最有信息量。

Method: CFNBC采用离线数据选择框架：首先在干净演示上训练初始策略；然后构造配对干扰观测（保持专家动作不变）；计算动作漂移（干扰前后策略预测动作的变化）作为策略特定的敏感性信号；据此从更大候选池中选择紧凑且响应多样的修复集，无需回滚成功标签或在线策略执行。

Result: 在MuJoCo双臂方块搬运和SimplerEnv方块堆叠任务中，动作漂移与干扰导致的失败相关；响应引导修复仅用20-30个选中候选就大幅超越同预算随机选择，并接近更大随机修复预算的性能。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性修复应基于数据中心的视角：最有用的数据不是最多、视觉最多样或明显困难的，而是覆盖当前策略脆弱响应模式的那些示例。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation learning has demonstrated success for manipulation tasks. However, the trained policies remain brittle to visual `nuisances', with even minor task-preserving variations such as lighting, distractions or changes in colour result in heavy degradation of the trained policy's performance. While increasing data diversity can improve robustness, it is unclear which additional demonstrations are informative for a particular trained policy. We propose Counterfactual Nuisance Behaviour Cloning (CFNBC), an offline data-selection framework for targeted robustness repair. Starting from a nominal policy trained on `clean' demonstrations, CFNBC generates paired clean and nuisance observations that preserve the expert action, then measures \emph{action drift}: the change in the policy's predicted action under a nuisance that should not alter the desired behaviour. This provides a policy-specific sensitivity signal for selecting a compact, response-diverse repair set from a larger candidate pool, without requiring rollout success labels or online policy execution. We show in MuJoCo bimanual cube transfer and SimplerEnv cube stacking that action drift correlates with nuisance-induced failure, and that response-guided repair with only $20$--$30$ selected candidates substantially outperforms matched-budget random selection while approaching the performance of much larger random repair budgets. These results support a data-centric view of robustness repair: the most useful data are not necessarily the most numerous, visually diverse, or obviously difficult, but the examples that cover fragile response modes of the current policy.

</details>


### [342] [Simulation of Surgical Suturing Using Position-Based Dynamics and the Material Point Method for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27494)
*Tleukhan Mussin,Yafei Ou,Mahdi Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PBD模拟缝线和MPM模拟软组织的缝合仿真环境，实现了两者的双向接触耦合，并提供了GPU并行加速和强化学习训练环境，在针穿入和拔出任务中分别达到80%和68%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术机器人研究需要高性能仿真器，但完整软组织缝合场景（包括缝线与可变形组织的接触）缺乏有效的仿真方案。现有研究大多独立模拟缝线或软组织，很少有工作处理缝合过程中的双向接触。

Method: 采用基于位置的动力学（PBD）建模缝线，材料点法（MPM）建模软组织，引入PBD-MPM间的接触耦合方法以考虑摩擦力和拖曳力的双向接触。仿真器针对GPU优化，利用多CUDA流支持并行场景，并基于ML-Agents构建强化学习环境用于自主缝合子任务（针插入、穿引、拔出）训练。

Result: 强化学习代理在仿真器中表现出稳定的学习过程，在针插入和拔出任务中，在最严格的距离阈值下分别取得80%和68%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该缝合仿真环境能够在视觉上合理地呈现缝线与软组织的交互，支持GPU高效仿真及RL训练，为自主外科缝合机器人研究提供了可用平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in robotics research have created a strong demand for high-performance simulators. Surgical robotics simulation faces unique challenges due to the need to model diverse objects, such as rigid instruments, soft tissue, and fluids. While many studies simulate sutures or soft tissue independently, only a few have considered the complete soft-tissue suturing scenario, including the contact between sutures and deformable tissue during suture insertion. Building on previous work, this paper presents a novel suturing simulation environment using sutures modelled by position-based dynamics (PBD) and soft bodies modelled by the material point method (MPM) while considering two-way contact with frictional and drag forces. We introduce a contact coupling method between the PBD suture and the MPM soft tissue, enabling visually plausible suture-tissue interactions. The simulator is optimized for GPU execution with parallel scenes using multiple CUDA streams, and we present a Reinforcement Learning (RL) environment for autonomous suturing sub-tasks, including needle insertion, driving, and extraction. Using ML-Agents, RL agents trained in the simulator show stable learning and achieve 80% and 68% success rates in needle insertion and extraction, respectively, under the strictest distance threshold.

</details>


### [343] [Corrigible Assistance in One Round: Pragmatic-Pedagogic Best Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27508)
*Elle Lazarski,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究援助博弈（assistance games）中的人机协作问题，识别出一类可在单时间步内通过实用-教学推理（pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning）消解目标不确定性的博弈类型，使得全时域博弈可以通过可计算的best-response过程精确求解。文章进一步证明主流逆最优控制（inverse optimal control）在该类博弈中存在推理上限，阻碍人机对齐，而实用-教学推理可通过动作歧义消解克服此障碍。最终在积木协作示例上验证了理论结果与所提方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 援助博弈中，由于人类知道目标而机器人需要从观测和交互中推断目标，一般情况下的最优策略计算因需要在POMDP中规划而难以在线求解。作者旨在找到一类可精确且可高效求解的援助博弈子类，并揭示主流逆最优控制方法在此类问题中的局限。

Method: 作者识别了一类特殊援助博弈，其中实用-教学推理（即人类教学式行动与机器人实用式推理相结合）能在单时间步内完全消除目标不确定性，从而使全时域决策退化为可解的best-response过程。通过对比分析，展示了逆最优控制在推理上的天花板，并给出超越该限制的推理框架。

Result: 在该类援助博弈中，所求方法实现精确求解且计算可处理；相比之下，主流逆最优控制存在推理上限，无法在此类场景中实现有效的目标对齐。在积木搭建示例中，所提方法成功通过具有教学意图的动作立即消解歧义并完成协作。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上刻画了一类可精确求解的援助博弈，论证了实用-教学推理优于传统逆最优控制的可行性，并为高效人机协作策略设计提供了新的理论依据和算法路径。

Abstract: Assistance games formalize human-robot collaboration under asymmetric information: the human knows the goal, while the robot must infer it from observation and interaction in order to assist effectively. In general, computing optimal assistance game strategies online is intractable, since exact solutions require planning in a POMDP. We identify a class of assistance games in which pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning resolves goal uncertainty in a single time step, rendering the full-horizon game exactly solvable by a tractable best-response procedure. Within this class, we show that mainstream inverse optimal control exhibits an inference ceiling that hinders alignment, while pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning overcomes this barrier by immediately disambiguating goals through actions that look equivalent under task execution alone. Finally, we validate our theoretical results and proposed method on a simple collaborative block-building example.

</details>


### [344] [Failure Detection for Surgical Robot Imitation Policies via Flow-Matching World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27511)
*Zhefeng Huang,Yilin Cai,Ankit Patel,Mohammad Hajiha,Brendan Browne,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FoMo-FD，一种基于流匹配世界模型的机器人手术失败检测方法，通过学习正常短视距视觉动力学并结合保形校准设定检测阈值，无需失败演示数据即可实现窗口级别的视觉-动作一致性异常检测，在dVRK上的四项手术任务、二十种失败模式中取得了96.6%的失败检出率和1.3%的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人的模仿学习虽前景广阔，但手术任务的安全关键特性及环境复杂性使得安全部署极具挑战。失败检测作为必要保障，面临三大难点：失败数据稀缺、操作动力学高度多变、以及需要在漏检与误报之间取得平衡。因此需要一种无需失败演示数据即可可靠检测异常的方法。

Method: FoMo-FD采用动作条件化的流匹配世界模型学习正常短视距视觉动力学，对观测到的终点潜在表示计算逆输运非一致性得分，从而在窗口级别检测视觉-动作不一致性。检测阈值通过成功执行轨迹上的保形校准获得，可生成任务特定的告警阈值，无需假设未来的失败类型。

Result: 在da Vinci研究套件（dVRK）上的四项手术相关操作任务及二十种失败模式的模拟和真实实验中，FoMo-FD优于观测级别的异常检测基线和同世界模型的预测误差变体，其中腕部相机视角表现最佳，达到了96.6%的失败检出率（FDR）和1.3%的误报率（FAR）。

Conclusion: FoMo-FD证明了基于流匹配世界模型的非一致性评分可有效用于手术机器人的失败检测，无需失败样本即可实现高检出率和低误报率，为安全关键的手术机器人自主操作提供了可靠的防护机制。

Abstract: Imitation learning has shown increasing promise for autonomous robotic surgery, yet safe deployment remains challenging due to the safety-critical nature of surgical tasks and the complexity and variability of surgical environments. Failure detection is therefore an essential safeguard, but its development remains difficult due to the challenges of scarce failure data, highly variable manipulation dynamics, and the need to balance missed detections against disruptive false alarms. To address these challenges, we introduce FoMo-FD (Flow-Matching World Model for Failure Detection), a failure detection method that learns nominal short-horizon visual dynamics with an action-conditioned flow-matching world model. FoMo-FD scores the inverse-transport nonconformity of observed endpoint latents, enabling window-level detection of visual-action inconsistencies without requiring failure demonstrations. Detection thresholds are obtained by conformal calibration on successful executions, yielding task-specific alarms without assuming future failure types. We evaluate FoMo-FD on four surgically relevant manipulation tasks with twenty failure modes across simulation and real-world experiments using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). Results show that FoMo-FD outperforms observation-level anomaly baselines and a prediction-error variant of the same world model, with the wrist-camera view achieving the strongest performance, including a 96.6% failure detection rate (FDR) at a 1.3% false alarm rate (FAR).

</details>


### [345] [Cross-Embodiment Transfer via Behavior-Aligned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27549)
*Ajay Sridhar,Jensen Gao,Jonathan Yang,Jean Mercat,Suneel Belkhale,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究行为对齐表示（如物体边界框、语言动作、末端执行器轨迹）在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的角色，以促进跨具身（cross-embodiment）迁移。作者构建了基于仿真的基准测试，发现末端执行器轨迹表示对迁移尤为有效，表示在大规模先验数据集下更有用，且可利用无动作数据；该方法将仿真到真实的跨具身迁移中真实机器人任务完成进度提升了28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人操作模仿学习已通过跨多种机器人具身的数据集取得进展，但显著的跨具身迁移仍然具有挑战性。作者希望探索行为对齐表示是否能通过在不同具身间具备不变性、同时又能预测机器人动作，来统一大规模跨具身数据，从而增强迁移能力。

Method: 提出假设：行为对齐表示（具备跨具身不变性且能预测动作）有助于跨具身迁移。为此开发了基于仿真的基准测试，用于评估多样跨具身数据到新具身的迁移能力；在该基准上比较不同表示（如物体边界框、语言动作、末端执行器轨迹）以及不同的融合方式。

Result: 研究发现：末端执行器轨迹对迁移尤其有益；表示为较大规模先验数据集带来的增益更明显；这些表示还可用于利用无动作数据。此外，这些表示能增强仿真到真实的跨具身迁移，将基于仿真数据预训练的机器人策略的真实任务完成进度提升28%。

Conclusion: 行为对齐表示，尤其是末端执行器轨迹，是促进VLA模型跨具身迁移的有效途径，能在统一跨具身数据、利用无动作数据及提升仿真到真实迁移方面发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale imitation learning for robot manipulation has been driven by leveraging datasets across a wide range of robot embodiments. However, achieving significant cross-embodiment transfer is often still challenging. In this work, we study the role of using behavior-aligned representations (e.g., object bounding boxes, language motions, end-effector traces of robot motion) in vision-language-action (VLA) models to promote cross-embodiment transfer. We hypothesize that by possessing invariances across embodiments while being predictive of robot actions, these representations can help unify large-scale cross-embodiment data to enhance transfer. To assess our hypothesis, we develop a simulation-based benchmark designed to assess transfer with diverse cross-embodiment data to new embodiments. Using this benchmark, we compare different representations and ways of incorporating them. We identify that end-effector traces can be particularly beneficial for transfer, representations are generally more useful with larger prior datasets, and can be used to benefit from action-free data. We also demonstrate that they can enhance sim-to-real cross-embodiment transfer, improving task completion progress of real robot policies pre-trained on simulation data by 28%. We provide videos of our evaluations at our website: https://ajaysridhar.com/barx/.

</details>


### [346] [A Systems Engineering Framework for Vision-Language-Enabled UAV Triage and Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27597)
*Swapnil Saha,Bhuvan Rajanasiriyur Jagadeesha,Karishma Patnaik,Neelakshi Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索将视觉语言模型（VLM）嵌入人-无人机回路中作为协调智能体，而非仅作决策支持工具。该架构整合自然语言交互、任务级协调、软件在环实现及ICS通信协议，初步人因评估（七名参与者）显示可降低感知工作负荷并提升AI信任度和通信清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在灾害响应中的应用主要限于决策支持角色（如社交媒体监测、草拟行动计划、翻译技术警报），这增加了操作员负担，因为人类仍需将输出转化为跨团队和机器人资产的协调行动。

Method: 采用基于模型的系统工程（MBSE）方法开发框架，利用用例图和块定义图表示系统角色、内部结构和组件交互；实现VLM协调智能体、无人机任务控制和任务分配器三个核心组件于集成仿真与控制环境中，并进行七名参与者的人因评估。

Result: 初步人因评估显示感知工作负荷在脑力需求、努力和挫败感方面有所降低，AI信任度和通信清晰度获得高评分。

Conclusion: 通过整合MBSE、软件在环测试和人因评估，本工作推进了高风险灾害响应中可扩展的人-自主系统协同，对航空航天自主性和民用安全具有更广泛的影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have created new opportunities for disaster response, where responders must interpret large volumes of sensor data under time pressure. Current VLM applications include social media monitoring for situational awareness, generation of draft action plans, and translation of technical alerts into public-facing messages. While these efforts can accelerate information flow, they remain largely limited to decision-support roles. Such approaches can increase operator burden because humans must still translate outputs into coordinated actions across teams and robotic assets. This study explores the viability of embedding VLMs as coordination agents within the human-UAV loop. The proposed architecture integrates natural language interaction, mission-level task coordination, software-in-the-loop implementation, and communication aligned with the Incident Command System (ICS). Rather than functioning solely as advisory tools, VLMs facilitate communication between human operators, mission control logic, and UAV task execution. The framework was developed using a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) approach, with use case and block definition diagrams representing system roles, internal structure, and component interactions. Three key elements, the VLM Coordinator Agent, UAV Mission Control, and Task Allocator, were implemented within an integrated simulation and control environment. A preliminary human-factors evaluation with seven participants showed reduced perceived workload across mental demand, effort, and frustration, along with high ratings for AI trust and communication clarity. By integrating MBSE, software-in-the-loop testing, and human-factors evaluation, this work advances scalable human-autonomy teaming for high-stakes disaster response, with broader implications for aerospace autonomy and civil safety.

</details>


### [347] [Arm2Air: Cross-Embodiment Skeleton Transfer for 3D Relay Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27627)
*Dohun Lee,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Seokmin Kim,Byongho Lee,Seungjoo Oh,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Arm2Air方法，通过跨具身迁移（cross-embodiment transfer）将机器人手臂的避障骨架知识迁移到无人机中继部署任务中，利用预训练的Neural MP模型、Transformer迁移平台和Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA），在数据与计算受限条件下实现高效的无人机中继部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机中继网络可在通信基础设施受损后恢复连通性，但城市环境下的中继部署需同时考虑视距遮挡、通信范围、高度及三维障碍物等多种因素，传统规划器计算开销大且优化效果有限，亟需计算高效且数据高效的中继放置方法。

Method: 首先利用预训练的Neural MP模型生成源域（机器人手臂）的运动轨迹，将其转换为有序骨架序列；接着用这些骨架预训练基于Transformer的迁移平台；随后利用有限的无人机领域目标数据和Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）对模型进行领域适配；最后将迁移得到的骨架初始化中继链，并对连通性、瓶颈容量、时延和移动成本进行精炼优化。

Result: 在9个高密度杂乱3D城市地图上，Arm2Air相较最快的传统规划器将端到端规划运行时间中位数降低64.9%；在30幅密集城市地图的高遮挡组中，相较IMPC-MD提升瓶颈容量32.6%、降低容量方差74.7%、降低最大跳距13.2%、降低跳距方差75.2%、降低中继位移16.9%；仅用3张目标域训练地图，相比从零训练将中继位置均方根误差降低53.6%，仅更新0.134M参数（对比Scratch和全微调的1.383M参数）。

Conclusion: Arm2Air验证了跨具身迁移策略在异构具身任务中传递有序结构先验的有效性，同时显著提升计算与数据效率，为无人机中继部署提供了新的解决思路，并暗示了更广泛的跨具身迁移原则。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) relay networks can restore connectivity after communication infrastructure is damaged. Urban relay placement is difficult because line-of-sight blockage, communication range, altitude, and three-dimensional obstacles must be considered jointly. Arm2Air transfers obstacle-avoidance skeletons from robot arms to UAV relay placement through cross-embodiment transfer. Source-domain robot-arm motions from a pretrained Neural MP model are converted into ordered skeletons that pretrain a transformer-based transfer platform, which is then adapted to the UAV domain using limited target data and Low-Rank Adaptation. The transferred skeleton initializes a relay chain that is refined for connectivity, bottleneck capacity, delay, and movement cost. On nine held-out high-clutter 3D urban maps, Arm2Air reduced median end-to-end planning runtime by 64.9 percent relative to the fastest conventional planner. On the high-obstruction group of a separate 30-map dense urban holdout, it increased bottleneck capacity by 32.6 percent, reduced capacity variance by 74.7 percent, reduced maximum hop distance by 13.2 percent, reduced hop-distance variance by 75.2 percent, and reduced relay displacement by 16.9 percent relative to IMPC-MD. With only three target-domain training maps, Arm2Air reduced relay-position root mean square error by 53.6 percent relative to training from scratch while updating 0.134 million parameters, compared with 1.383 million for Scratch and Full Fine-tuning. These results demonstrate computationally and data-efficient UAV relay placement and suggest a broader principle for transferring ordered structural priors across heterogeneous embodied tasks.

</details>


### [348] [LabEvolver: Training-Free Experience Evolution for Safe and Grounded Wet-Lab Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27690)
*Jingya Wang,Yuyang Gao,Liuzhenghao Lv,Yonghong Tian,Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabEvolver是一个无需训练的框架，为安全且基于实际场景的湿实验室智能体配备从执行经验中获得的 episodic memory（情景记忆）。它通过内层试错循环（状态感知、在线规划、安全验证）和外层进化循环（将完成的轨迹提炼为可复用的技能、策略和安全经验）相结合，在机器人溶液配制任务中减少48.2%的pH调节完成时间和60.0%的安全闸拦截率，在ALFWorld基准上将20步内累计成功率从76.2%提升至91.4%，验证了"干中学"经验进化作为闭环自动化科学发现的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的湿实验室智能体缺乏从执行经验中持续学习和积累的能力，导致每次任务都需要从头规划，效率和安全保障不足。作者旨在通过经验进化的方式，让智能体能够在实际执行中不断积累可复用的技能、策略和安全经验，从而提升任务的完成效率和安全性。

Method: LabEvolver采用双循环结构：(1) 内层试错循环（inner trial loop）——基于状态感知的适应性感知、在线规划和安全验证；(2) 外层进化循环（outer evolution loop）——将已完成轨迹提炼为可复用的技能（skill）、策略（strategy）和安全经验（safety experience），形成episodic memory。整个框架无需额外训练（training-free），直接利用执行经验驱动进化。

Result: 在机器人溶液配制任务中，pH调节完成时间降低48.2%，安全闸拦截率降低60.0%；在ALFWorld基准上，500个连续任务中20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升至91.4%，展示了超越湿实验室场景的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，"干中学"（learn-by-doing）的经验进化机制是可行的，能够支撑闭环自动化科学发现，为湿实验室智能体乃至更广泛的具身智能体提供了可扩展的经验积累范式。

Abstract: We introduce LabEvolver, a training-free framework that equips safe and grounded wet-lab agents with episodic memory from execution experience. LabEvolver couples a state-grounded inner trial loop for adaptive perception, online planning, and safety validation with an outer evolution loop that distills completed trajectories into reusable skill, strategy, and safety experience. On robotic solution-preparation tasks, LabEvolver demonstrates real-world feasibility, reducing pH-regulation completion time and safety-gate intercepts by 48.2% and 60.0%, respectively. On ALFWorld, it further improves cumulative success rate within 20 steps from 76.2% with ReAct to 91.4% over 500 continual tasks, showing generality beyond wet-lab settings. These results support learn-by-doing experience evolution as a feasible path toward closed-loop automated scientific discovery. The project page is available at https://github.com/AndyGao6186/LabEvolver.

</details>


### [349] [Write-Safe Flow Field Mapping under Ambiguous Onboard Sensing and Localization Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27713)
*Linhao Jin,Qimin Feng,Peter Gunnarson,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种地图参考感知的保守融合框架，通过学习写入安全分数来衰减不确定的地图更新，从而减少移动机器人流场建图中的幽灵结构污染，在合成和真实实验中分别降低42%和39%的幽灵污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人从机载传感推断局部流场结构时，局部合理的估计不一定能安全写入全局地图。相似的流场结构可能产生模糊观测，而定位漂移导致预测片段被写入错误位置，重复的错误注册更新会累积成持久的幽灵结构，损害地图质量。

Method: 提出地图参考感知的保守融合框架。模型预测局部速度场片段和一个学习得到的写入安全分数，该分数持续衰减不确定的地图更新，同时在没有可靠地图参考时允许初始化。通过在线学习的方式将安全分数与地图参考相结合，实现对写入操作的门控。

Result: 在合成射流和横流环境中，所提方法相对无门控融合平均减少42%的幽灵污染。零样本硬件回放实验使用真实压力和光流测量数据（推进器尾流），额外减少39%幽灵污染，同时保留81%的地图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 安全地图写入对于在模糊感知和定位漂移条件下的流场建图至关重要，所提出的保守融合框架能有效抑制幽灵结构的累积，是流场建图的关键环节。

Abstract: Mobile robots can infer local flow structure from onboard sensing, but a locally plausible estimate is not always safe to write into a global map. Similar flow structures may produce ambiguous observations, while localization drift causes predicted patches to be written at incorrect locations. Repeated misregistered updates then accumulate into persistent ghost structures. We address this failure mode with a map-reference-aware conservative fusion framework. The model predicts a local velocity patch and a learned write-safety score that continuously attenuates uncertain map updates while permitting initialization when no reliable map reference is available. Across synthetic jet and crossflow environments, the proposed method reduces average ghost contamination by 42% relative to ungated fusion. A zero-shot hardware replay using real pressure and optical-flow measurements from a thruster wake further reduces ghost contamination by 39% while retaining 81% map coverage. These results show that safe map writing is critical for flow mapping under ambiguous sensing and localization drift.

</details>


### [350] [RedFlow: Redirect Failure into Action-Level Corrections for Flow-matching VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27782)
*Zhengyang Yan,Junhao Li,Fangqi Zhu,Zijun Wang,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Xiaoyi Pang,Zicong Hong,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedFlow是一个面向flow-matching视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略的细粒度离线强化学习框架，通过将失败经验转化为动作级别的纠正性监督信号，来应对部署时分布偏移导致的累积误差问题。在LIBERO基准和三类真实世界操作任务中，RedFlow显著超越现有最优离线RL基线，将真实世界成功率从56.7%提升至74.7%，并达到与强在线方法(PPO、GRPO、DDPO)相当的性能，同时训练样本需求约低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-matching VLA策略在机器人操作中表现优异，但部署时因分布偏移会产生累积误差(compounding errors)。离线RL是改善已部署策略的实用手段，但现有方法要么忽略失败数据，要么仅在轨迹层面利用失败数据，导致学习效率低且错误持续存在。

Method: RedFlow包含两个核心组件：(1) 上下文感知纠正匹配(Context-Aware Corrective Matching)——识别导致失败的特定动作，并从相似上下文中检索成功的替代动作作为纠正目标；(2) 自适应纠正目标(Adaptive Redirection Objective)——联合强化成功动作、抑制不良动作，并将可恢复的失败动作重定向至纠正目标。该方法将成功与失败经验均转化为稠密监督信号，实现从混合质量数据中的鲁棒恢复学习。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和三个真实世界操作任务上，RedFlow持续超越现有最优离线RL基线，将真实世界成功率从56.7%提升至74.7%，并匹配甚至超越强在线方法(PPO、GRPO、DDPO)的性能，同时仅需约其十分之一的训练样本。

Conclusion: 通过将失败轨迹中的具体动作识别并重定向至正确的上下文匹配目标，RedFlow实现了对混合质量数据的稠密监督学习，有效解决了flow-matching VLA策略在离线RL中因粗粒度利用失败数据而导致的学习效率低下和错误持续问题。

Abstract: Flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation but often suffer from compounding errors caused by distribution shifts during deployment. While offline reinforcement learning (RL) provides a practical way to improve deployed policies using rollout data, existing methods either ignore failure data or exploit it only at the trajectory level, resulting in low learning efficiency and persistent errors. We propose **RedFlow**, a fine-grained offline RL framework that redirects failure experiences into action-level corrective supervision for flow-matching VLA policies. RedFlow consists of two key components: (1) a **Context-Aware Corrective Matching** mechanism that identifies failure-inducing actions and retrieves successful alternatives from similar contexts as corrective targets, and (2) an **Adaptive Redirection Objective** that jointly reinforces successful actions, suppresses undesirable ones, and redirects recoverable failures toward corrective targets. By converting both successful and failed experiences into dense supervision, RedFlow enables robust recovery learning from mixed-quality data. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark and three real-world manipulation tasks show that RedFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art offline RL baselines, improving the real-world success rate from 56.7% to 74.7%. It also matches strong on-policy methods (PPO, GRPO, and DDPO) while requiring roughly an order of magnitude fewer training samples.

</details>


### [351] [DexDirect: Direct Kinesthetic Arm Guidance for Efficient Dexterous Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27784)
*Beom Jun Kim,Shiu-Jen Wang,Jonathan Liu,Alvin Zhu,Quanyou Wang,Hanzhang Fang,Feng Xu,Mingzhang Zhu,Yuchen Cui,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDirect提出了一种结合6-DoF重力补偿机器人手臂直接力引导和单摄像头手部重定向的灵巧操作示教采集接口，相比纯视觉和姿态跟踪基线分别多采集17.2倍和3.2倍的成功演示，训练出的扩散策略在立方体抓取放置任务上达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作演示数据的可扩展采集是机器人学习的主要瓶颈。现有高保真接口成本高、设置复杂，而低成本替代方案控制精度低且认知负担重，需要一种兼具低成本、易设置和高效率的示教采集方案。

Method: DexDirect采用双通道设计：(1) 操作者通过手柄直接拖拽6-DoF重力补偿机器人手臂进行手臂运动示教；(2) 单个webcam实时重定向操作者另一只手到16关节13-DoF的灵巧机械手上。两者结合实现手臂与手部的协同控制。

Result: 用户研究表明：相比纯视觉基线（AnyTeleop）和姿态追踪基线（TeleDex），DexDirect分别采集到17.2倍和3.2倍的成功演示数量。改良NASA-TLX量表显示DexDirect显著降低了心理需求、努力和挫败感，但增加了身体需求。基于DexDirect演示数据训练的扩散策略在立方体抓取放置任务上达到90%成功率。

Conclusion: 直接力引导的机械臂控制结合视觉手部重定向是一种高效、低设置成本且可扩展的灵巧操作演示采集接口，在采集效率、操作者负荷和学习效果之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Scalable collection of dexterous manipulation demonstrations remains a major bottleneck for robot learning. High-fidelity interfaces often require costly hardware and extensive setup, while low-setup, low cost alternatives tend to provide less precise control and impose greater cognitive workload on operators. We present DexDirect, a direct kinesthetic arm guidance for efficient dexterous demonstration collection. The operator drags a 6-DoF gravity-compensated robot arm directly by a handle, while a single webcam retargets operator's other hand onto a 16 joints 13-DoF dexterous robot hand. User studies suggest DexDirect collects 17.2x and 3.2x more successful demonstrations compared to purely vision (AnyTeleop) and pose-tracking (TeleDex) baselines. An adapted NASA-TLX shows DexDirect greatly reduces mental demand, effort, and frustration, despite raising physical demand. A diffusion policy trained on DexDirect demonstrations reaches a 90% success rate on a cube pick-and-place task. These results suggest that direct kinesthetic arm guidance combined with vision-based hand retargeting provides an efficient low-setup and scalable interface for collecting dexterous manipulation demonstrations

</details>


### [352] [RoboBRIDGE: A Modular Framework for Bridging Policies to Robust Real-World Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27881)
*Sihyung Yoon,Minjong Yoo,Sanghyun Ahn,Seojeong Choi,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboBRIDGE是一个模块化框架，通过五个协调模块（Monitor、Perceptor、Planner、Controller和Robot Interface）将预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型编排为可靠的机器人智能体，弥补了VLA模型在故障恢复、长时程执行一致性和领域鲁棒性方面的关键缺陷。在LIBERO、RoboCasa和真实世界案例中，RoboBRIDGE稳定优于独立策略和先前的增强型VLA部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽然是有效的动作预测器，但直接部署为机器人智能体时存在三个关键缺口：缺乏故障恢复机制、长时程执行中行为不一致、对观测/任务/实体变化的鲁棒性有限。现有方法仅针对单个问题进行模型重训练或环境特定模块设计，缺乏一个通用的系统性框架。

Method: RoboBRIDGE采用模块化设计，在预训练VLA周围提供编排层，包含五个协调模块：Monitor负责快速故障检测并结合分层恢复机制纠正错误；Perceptor异步更新场景理解避免执行停滞；Planner在环境偏离当前计划时触发重新规划；Controller通过将操作分解为领域不变的基元技能并配备专用LoRA适配器进行微调，降低领域偏移敏感性；Robot Interface负责连接各模块与机器人实体。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa基准及覆盖多种机器人平台和VLA骨干网络的实际场景中，RoboBRIDGE一致性地优于独立的策略模型及此前增强型的VLA部署方案。

Conclusion: 可靠的机器人智能体能力并非仅仅来自扩大动作预测器的规模，而是来自围绕这些预测器的结构化编排。RoboBRIDGE证明了利用现成组件（包括预训练VLA）可以系统性地构建鲁棒的机器人智能体。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have attracted growing interest as a scalable approach to robotic manipulation. While these models are effective action predictors, deploying them as robotic agents exposes critical gaps: no mechanism for failure recovery, inconsistent execution over long horizons, and limited robustness to shifts in observations, tasks, or embodiments. Existing solutions address these limitations individually through model retraining or environment-specific modules, yet what is needed is a general framework that systematically transforms a pretrained VLA into a robotic agent. We present RoboBRIDGE, a modular framework that provides an orchestration layer over five coordinated modules, namely Monitor, Perceptor, Planner, Controller, and Robot Interface, to compose robust robotic agents from off-the-shelf components, including pretrained VLAs. The Monitor pairs rapid failure detection with hierarchical recovery to correct errors before they cascade. When the environment diverges from the current plan, the Planner triggers replanning while the Perceptor updates scene understanding asynchronously, avoiding execution stalls. Within the Controller, primitive skill fine-tuning factors manipulation into domain-invariant primitives with dedicated LoRA adapters, reducing sensitivity to domain shifts when a VLA is used. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world case studies spanning multiple robot platforms and VLA backbones, RoboBRIDGE consistently outperforms both standalone policies and prior augmented VLA deployments. These results suggest that reliable robotic agency does not arise from scaling action predictors alone, but from structured orchestration around them.

</details>


### [353] [Static In, Dynamic Out: Counterfactual Action Augmentation for Moving Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27890)
*Woo Chul Shin,Zhenyang Chen,Alfred Cueva,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Yingyan Celine Lin,Benjamin Joffe,Shreyas Kousik,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIDO是一种反事实动作增强方法，使仅在静态物体演示上训练的视觉运动策略能够适应测试时的物体运动。它通过将静态操作分解为"预测物体未来位置"和"到达预测位姿"两个子问题，核心思�path在训练时把物体转移到反事实的未来位置并变形演示动作块以保持手-物相对位姿，从而获得目标条件化策略。在三个模拟任务和两个真实任务的五类运动模式下，SIDO在保持静态性能的同时显著提升了运动物体操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略主要针对执行过程中目标物体保持静止的操作任务，但真实部署中物体常常处于运动状态（如传送带上的零件漂移、风中摇摆的水果），这打破了静态假设，导致策略失效。目标是在不使用运动物体演示数据的前提下，让静态训练的策略适应未知的物体运动。

Method: SIDO将运动物体操作分解为两个子问题：(1) 预测物体未来的位置；(2) 到达预测的位姿。核心方案是反事实动作增强（counterfactual action augmentation）：训练时将物体位移到反事实的未来位置，并将演示的动作块进行变换（morph），保持手与物体之间的相对位姿不变，从而生成目标条件化的策略。部署时使用物体位姿预测器提供未来的目标位置。

Result: 在三个模拟任务（Mug、Square、Stack）的五种物体运动模式以及两个真实任务（Gantry、Peachtree）上，SIDO相比基线方法在运动物体操作上取得了更高的成功率，同时保持了静态物体的原有性能不退化。

Conclusion: SIDO通过在训练阶段进行反事实动作增强，将静态演示策略泛化到动态物体场景，证明了无需运动数据即可实现运动物体的鲁棒操作，为静态到动态的视觉运动策略迁移提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies have advanced on manipulation tasks where the target object stays static during execution, but real deployments break this assumption: parts drift on conveyors and fruits sway in the wind. We introduce Static In, Dynamic Out (SIDO), a counterfactual action augmentation that enables a policy trained only on static object demonstrations to adapt to unseen object motion at test time. Our key idea is to factorize moving object manipulation into two sub-problems: predicting where the object will be, and reaching that predicted pose. SIDO displaces the object to a counterfactual future position and morphs the demonstrated action chunk to preserve the hand-object relative pose, yielding a goal-conditioned policy. At deployment an object pose predictor supplies the future position. Across three simulated tasks (Mug, Square, Stack) under five object motion patterns and two real-world tasks (Gantry, Peachtree), SIDO improves moving object success over the baselines while preserving static object performance. Project website: https://sido-staticindynamicout.github.io/.

</details>


### [354] [Learning Social Robot Navigation By Sensing Human Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27922)
*Alberto Vaglio,Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Tommaso Van Der Meer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CALF（卷积注意力腿部特征）架构，是一种端到端神经网络，直接从2D LiDAR扫描中解读行人腿部运动并生成安全导航命令。配套开发了LegNav轻量级仿真器，融合LiDAR射线追踪与新型行人步态模型，支持深度强化学习训练。在仿真和真实TurtleBot 4平台上均验证了平滑且符合社会规范的轨迹，且训练时间在单张消费级GPU上不足一小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在行人环境中多使用贴近地面的2D LiDAR进行感知，该高度传感器主要观测到行人腿部而非完整人体，但大多数基于学习的导航方法仍将行人视为圆形等简单几何形状，忽略了对腿部特征的利用，导致感知与建模之间存在显著差距。

Method: CALF采用卷积层、注意力机制与MLP结合的网络架构，端到端地从LiDAR扫描中直接解释腿部运动特征。训练使用深度强化学习算法，在自研LegNav仿真器中进行，该仿真器结合2D LiDAR射线追踪与新颖的行人步态模型，整体以JAX编写实现。

Result: CALF策略在导航性能和社会规范符合度上优于经典与传统学习基线方法；通过TurtleBot 4上的零样本部署在真实世界实验中验证，产生平滑且社会合规的轨迹；训练效率高，在单张消费级GPU上不足一小时即完成部署级策略训练。

Conclusion: 从腿部特征直接学习导航策略是可行且有效的，弥补了低高度LiDAR感知与简化行人建模之间的鸿沟，且LegNav的高效仿真环境使训练成本大幅降低，为真实世界部署提供了实用的端到端导航方案。

Abstract: Robots navigating among pedestrians typically sense their surroundings with a 2D LiDAR mounted close to the ground. At that height, the sensor mostly sees moving legs rather than whole people, yet most learning-based navigation methods still treat pedestrians as simple shapes like circles. This paper addresses that gap with CALF (Convolutional Attention for Leg Features), an end-to-end neural architecture that combines convolutional layers, attention, and MLP to interpret leg motion directly from LiDAR scans and produce safe navigation commands. The CALF policy is trained using deep reinforcement learning algorithms within LegNav, a custom lightweight 2D simulator that combines 2D LiDAR ray tracing with a novel pedestrian gait model. The resulting policy is compared against classical and learning-based baselines in terms of navigation performance and social compliance. The approach is validated through real-world experiments via zero-shot deployment on a TurtleBot 4, yielding smooth and socially compliant trajectories. Written in JAX, the LegNav simulator enables the training of a deployment-ready CALF policy in under an hour on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [355] [RaDiVe: Robust 4D Radar Odometry with Distance-Bounded NDT and Velocity-Discrepancy Point Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28045)
*Sangwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,Chiyun Noh,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RaDiVe是一个4D雷达里程计框架，通过距离受限NDT、速度差异点不确定性模型和基于SDF的隐式神经映射表面点提取，显著提升了雷达点云配准的精度与鲁棒性，在公共数据集上平均降低平移ATE 44.4%、旋转ATE 21.3%，且保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管4D雷达在恶劣天气下具有鲁棒感知能力，但其点云固有的稀疏性、噪声和有限的位置精度给基于配准的里程计带来了显著挑战，现有方法难以在精度和鲁棒性上达到理想效果。

Method: 1) 提出距离受限的Normal Distributions Transform (NDT)，将对应搜索限制在近邻体素对以提升优化稳定性和计算效率；2) 提出速度差异点不确定性模型，根据测量多普勒径向速度与估计自车速度预测的径向速度之间的差异为每个点加权，以缓解测量模糊性；3) 引入基于隐式神经映射的SDF表面点提取，构建几何一致且噪声过滤的局部子图。

Result: 在多个公共数据集上的评估表明，RaDiVe相比现有4D雷达里程计基线，平均降低平移ATE 44.4%、旋转ATE 21.3%，同时保持实时性能。

Conclusion: RaDiVe通过结合距离受限NDT、速度差异加权和SDF神经表面提取，有效解决了4D雷达点云配准中的稀疏性、噪声和模糊性问题，显著提升了里程计精度，并将开源代码以促进机器人社区发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D radar enable robust perception in adverse weather; however, the inherent sparsity, noise, and limited positional precision of radar point clouds pose significant challenges for registration-based odometry. In this letter, we propose RaDiVe, a 4D radar odometry framework designed to improve the accuracy and robustness of radar point-cloud registration. We introduce a distance-bounded Normal Distributions Transform (NDT), which improves optimization stability and computational efficiency by restricting the correspondence search to near-distance voxel pairs. To mitigate measurement ambiguity, we propose a velocity-discrepancy point uncertainty model that weights each input 4D radar point according to the discrepancy between its measured Doppler radial velocity and the radial velocity predicted from the estimated ego-velocity. Furthermore, we incorporate Signed Distance Function (SDF)-based surface point extraction via implicit neural mapping to construct a geometrically consistent and noise-filtered local submap. Evaluations across multiple public datasets demonstrate that RaDiVe outperforms existing 4D radar odometry baselines by 44.4% in translational Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and 21.3% in rotational ATE on average, while maintaining real-time performance. The source code will be made publicly available to the robotics community: https://github.com/to-be-open-sourced.

</details>


### [356] [UniCross: Unified Cross-Skill Dexterous Manipulation Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28198)
*Hui Zhang,Julian Ferchow,Jie Song,Mirko Meboldt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将灵巧操作的四种核心技能（抓取、搬运、手内旋转、手内平移）建模为单一公式，共享相同的状态空间、动作空间和通用目标结构，从而实现跨技能策略蒸馏、长时程操作链式组合以及跨手型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将抓取、搬运、手内旋转和手内平移这四种灵巧操作技能分别建模，各自使用特定的动作约束、目标函数甚至专用的手部形态，破坏了长时程任务中技能组合所需的兼容性和连续性。作者希望揭示这些技能背后的内在一致性，构建一个统一的建模框架。

Method: 提出一个统一公式，将所有四种技能纳入同一框架，共享相同的状态空间、动作空间和共同的目标结构。该公式允许直接蒸馏出单一的跨技能策略，能够同时完成所有四种技能，且无需针对每种技能单独设计约束或目标。

Result: 统一策略在每种技能上都表现强劲，能泛化到未见过的物体，对扰动具有鲁棒性，并能无缝地将技能链接为长时程操作任务。该框架还能在不同手部形态之间有效迁移。

Conclusion: 不同的灵巧操作技能可以被视为一个共享任务公式的不同实例化，揭示了不同行为之间的内在一致性，为灵巧操作的统一学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: Many dexterous manipulation tasks require the object to remain securely held throughout the interaction. From the perspective of hand-object relational motion, such manipulation comprises four canonical skills: grasping, relocation, in-hand rotation, and in-hand translation. Human hands flexibly compose these skills to accomplish complex tasks. Existing approaches, however, model these skills separately with skill-specific action constraints, objectives, or even dedicated hand morphologies, which breaks the compatibility and continuity required for long-horizon composition. In this work, we present a unified framework that models all four skills in a single formulation that shares the same state and action spaces and a common objective structure. This formulation enables straightforward distillation of a single cross-skill policy that performs strongly on every skill, generalizes to unseen objects, stays robust to disturbances, and chains skills seamlessly into long-horizon manipulation. The framework also transfers effectively across different hand morphologies. Overall, our results suggest that different dexterous manipulation skills can be viewed as instantiations of a shared task formulation, revealing the intrinsic consistency across different behaviors.

</details>


### [357] [When Robots Exchange Meaning: A Demo of Goal-Oriented Semantic Communications for Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28256)
*Peizheng Li,Xinyi Lin,Sajida Gufran,Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个机器人-边缘语义通信（SemCom）测试平台，集成了机器人端视觉压缩、边缘端语义建图和可视化交互，通过VQ-VAE令牌化实现42.67倍压缩比，为面向任务的6G网络研究提供了端到端的演示平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人作为6G典型垂直用例，其通信质量应以任务执行和环境理解的效能来度量，而非仅以数据包投递率衡量。现有系统缺乏将语义通信、具身智能和物理AI相结合的端到端测试平台。

Method: 构建机器人-边缘语义通信测试平台：移动机器人基于ROS 2运行RGB-D感知和LiDAR，采用ONNX Runtime编码器将RGB帧压缩为VQ-VAE令牌；Jetson Orin边缘节点执行PyTorch解码器重建图像，并融合深度、位姿和RTAB-Map进行语义建图与物体处理；通过仪表盘实现浏览器端可视化与交互。320x240图像被编码为80x60令牌网格，打包载荷5400字节，相对模型输入RGB字节实现42.67倍压缩。

Result: 实现了42.67倍的图像压缩比，打通了从语义视觉传输入到物体级地图交互的完整链路，展示了语义通信在机器人-边缘协同场景下的可行性。

Conclusion: 该测试平台为未来面向任务的6G组网研究提供了实用平台，衔接SemCom、具身AI和物理AI赋能机器人三大方向。

Abstract: Collaborative robotics is a representative task-oriented 6G use-case, where communication quality should be reflected in mission execution, environment understanding, and closed-loop operation rather than packet delivery alone. This demo paper presents a robot-edge semantic communication (SemCom) testbed integrating robot-side visual compression, edge-side semantic mapping, and dashboard-based mission interaction. A mobile robot equipped with RGB-D sensing and LiDAR runs ROS 2, while a Jetson Orin edge node performs reconstruction, RTAB-Map mapping, semantic object handling, and browserbased visualization. As an initial proof of concept, RGB frames are encoded on the robot into VQ-VAE tokens using an ONNX Runtime encoder and reconstructed on the edge using a PyTorch decoder. A 320 X 240 image is represented by an 80 X 60 token grid with a packed payload of 5400 bytes, corresponding to a 42.67X reduction relative to model-input RGB bytes. The reconstructed visual stream is further associated with depth, pose, and 3D mapping information to generate a semantic map for downstream robotic applications. The demo exposes the full path from semantic visual transport to object-level map interaction, and provides a practical platform for future task-aware 6G networking studies at the intersection of SemCom, embodied AI, and physical AI-enabled robotics. A video of the demo is available at https://tinyurl.com/Tos09

</details>


### [358] [SemAnCorr: Semantic Anchored Correspondence for Zero-Shot Manipulation Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28382)
*Xiaoxiang Dong,William Baron,Hongyi Chen,Uksang Yoo,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SemAnCorr是一个无需训练的框架，通过联合姿态-对应关系优化选择语义一致的锚点区域，并利用函数映射在物体表面传播约束，建立既保持语义一致性又具备几何连贯性的密集对应关系，实现跨几何不同的物体实例的操作技能迁移，在PartNet-Mobility基准上达到90.8%的语义准确率，并显著提升零样本操作技能迁移的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨几何不同的同类物体实例迁移操作技能是机器人学习中的根本挑战。现有基于稠密视觉描述子和3D特征场的对应方法通过最近邻特征匹配，往往产生空间不连贯的对应关系，无法恢复技能迁移所需的局部几何坐标系，从而限制了技能迁移的可靠性。

Method: SemAnCorr采用训练自由（training-free）框架，核心步骤包括：(1) 通过联合姿态-对应关系优化选择语义一致的锚点区域；(2) 利用函数映射（functional maps）在物体表面上传播锚点约束；(3) 最终得到同时保持语义一致性与几何连贯性的稠密对应关系，支持以物体为中心的技能迁移。

Result: 在基于PartNet-Mobility构建的稠密对应基准上，SemAnCorr达到90.8%的语义准确率，并在几何连贯性上优于近期最先进的基线方法。在真实世界实验中，仅使用单个演示即可实现更可靠的零样本操作技能迁移到未见物体。

Conclusion: SemAnCorr证明了语义锚点选择与函数映射传播相结合能有效解决跨实例操作技能迁移中的对应问题，兼顾语义一致性与几何连贯性，为训练自由的技能迁移提供了可行且可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Transferring manipulation skills across object instances that share functionality but differ in geometry remains a fundamental challenge in robot learning. While recent correspondence methods leverage dense visual descriptors and 3D feature fields, nearest-neighbor feature matching often produces spatially incoherent correspondences that fail to recover the local geometric frames required for reliable skill transfer. We introduce SemAnCorr, a training-free framework that establishes dense correspondence by selecting semantically consistent anchor regions through joint pose-correspondence optimization and propagating these constraints over the object surface using functional maps. The resulting correspondences preserve both semantic consistency and geometric coherence, enabling object-centric manipulation skills to transfer across geometrically diverse instances. We evaluate SemAnCorr on a dense correspondence benchmark built on PartNet-Mobility, achieving 90.8% semantic accuracy in our benchmark evaluation while improving geometric coherence over recent state-of-the-art baselines. Finally, we show that these improvements translate directly into real-world manipulation performance: using a single demonstration, SemAnCorr enables substantially more reliable zero-shot manipulation skill transfer to previously unseen objects than existing correspondence methods. Videos and additional visualizations are available at [https://semancorr.github.io](https://semancorr.github.io) .

</details>


### [359] [TacWAM: Anchor-Guided World Action Model with Mechanics-Aware Tactile Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28391)
*Lei Jin,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacWAM是一个力学感知的触觉世界动作模型，通过空间对齐融合触觉编码器、触觉历史编码器和锚点引导三模态注意力，将触觉未来预测与机器人动作生成相结合，在四类接触丰富的真实操作任务上平均成功率75.0%，比最强基线高出37.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
